# The Loch Emperor
Aliases: Emperor, Loch Emperor, Sea Emperor, The Emperor, The Sea Emperor
Categories: Sea Creatures, Huntable Creatures, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Fishing, Mobs
The Loch Emperor arises from the depths.
[The Loch Emperor]

[Fishing Info]

The Loch Emperor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water on [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
The Loch Emperor can be fished up from water on [[Moonglade Marsh]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XX (20)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for The Loch Emperor is 100, resulting in a default catch chance of 1% (1 in 100 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase its weight:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|The Loch Emperor arises from the depths.|

The Loch Emperor can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Loch Emperor Shard]] and +500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.

## Trivia
- If the player brings a Loch Emperor using [[Necromancy]] to [[Jake]] in the [[Mushroom Desert]], they sell a [[Golden Ball]] for a limited time. It can be purchased from his shop for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
  - Capturing a Loch Emperor's soul will be displayed as The Emperor [Lv122].
- There was a bug with the [[Ink Wand]]'s ability Ink Bomb and the [[Explosive Bow]] Explosive Bow's explosive arrows, where if the Loch Emperor is killed by either of them, it could drop multiple [[Emperor's Skull|Emperor's Skulls]] and have a higher drop chance of rare items.
  - This was due to the Loch Emperor being technically two mobs. The abilities killed both of them at once, causing double drops. This was fixed in the Community Update, patch 0.9.
- The [[Blessing (Enchantment)|Blessing VI]] Enchantment Book drop was bugged from the Loch Emperor's loot table for a very long time until it was finally added with the [[Crimson Isle]] update.
- The Loch Emperor's previous [[Bestiary]] description implied the existence of at least seven seas, despite there currently being none.

ID: THUNDER_400
The Thunder is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from lava on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
Thunder can be fished up from any Lava source on the [[Crimson Isle]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXXVI (36)]] or higher to be fished up, as well as the [[Odger|Novice Trophy Fisher]] title.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Thunder is 40, resulting in a default catch chance of 0.86% (1 in 116.2 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|You hear a massive rumble as Thunder emerges.|
## Behavior
Thunder has 3 attacks, which all give [[Empty Thunder Bottle]] charges if they hit the player:
- Jumps into the air and quickly falls down, damaging players under it. (20 Charges)
- Shoots a laser at a nearby player, dealing 2,000 [[Damage]]. (10 Charges)
- Creates a circle of white and blue particles around a nearby player, then shoots a Sea Lantern at it. After 1 second, the Sea Lantern will land inside the circle and damage players for 15,000 [[Damage]] inside. (5 Charges)
When killed, Thunder explodes into 5-9 sparks. The amount depends on if the player has a [[Pulse Ring]], and its [[Rarity]], which increases the amount of sparks Thunder drops if the player is within 20 blocks of it when it dies. The sparks can be right-clicked on with an [[Empty Thunder Bottle]] to gain 250 Charges for that bottle per spark.

Thunder can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] to obtain [[Thunder Shard]] and +3,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.

Torrid is an EPIC [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water on [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Spawning
Torrid can be fished up from water on [[Torrhus Canyon]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing level XV (15)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Torrid is 500, resulting in a default catch chance of 5% (1 in 20 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|The laughter of a Torrid echoes through the air.|
## Behavior
The Torrid flies around the player after being caught. Using the rod held in its hand, it discharges blue particles similar to that of a [[Grim Reaper]]. The particles also pull the player toward the Torrid.

A Volcanic Snail is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished from [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspots]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
A Volcanic Snail can be fished up from any [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] in lava. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XVII (17)]] or higher to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Hotspot Tonic Mixin]]|+10%|
|[[Hotspot Hook]]|+20%|
|[[Hotspot Bait]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Hermit Crab Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Volcanic Snail is 2,875, resulting in a catch chance of 28.75% (1 in 3.48 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] pool. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|You feel a burning sensation as you reel in a Volcanic Snail!|
## Behavior
A Volcanic Snail very slowly moves towards the target player, leaving a trail of fire behind itself. A 5 radius ring of fire particles follows the Volcanic Snail, dealing [[True Damage]] to players within it 2 times a second.

## Spawning
A Water Hydra can be caught on the [[Hub Island]] and most other non-fishing islands. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XIX (19)]] or higher to be fished up.

The Water Hydra moves incredibly fast towards the player and attempt to hit them. Once it reaches 50% [[Health]], another Water Hydra spawns with 50% of its original [[Health]]. Both Water Hydras can drop items.

Water Hydra can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Water Hydra Shard]] and +500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.

## Trivia
- Since Water Hydras count as zombies, their Combat XP is enough to spawn a Tier V [[Revenant Horror]] given that another Water Hydra spawns below 50% of their [[Health|HP]].
- The name and ability of the Water Hydra are based on the Hydra in Greek mythology, which grows two additional heads each time one is severed.

Werewolf is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water one hour before, during, and after the [[Spooky Festival]].
## Spawning
Werewolf can be fished up from water one hour before, during, and after the [[Spooky Festival]], while the [[Fear Mongerer]] is in the [[Village]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XVII (17)]] to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Spooky Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Spooky Hook V Perk ([[Doug]])|+5%|
|[[Spooky Bait]]|+15%|
|[[Spooky Hook]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Bat Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Werewolf is 500, resulting in a catch chance of 5.00% (1 in 20 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Spooky Sea Creatures]] pool with all [[Sea Creatures]] unlocked.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|It must be a full moon, a Werewolf appears.|
## Behavior
Werewolves will run towards the player and attack them, dealing 250 [[Damage]]. The Werewolf's [[Damage]] increases by 20% for each second it is alive, capping at 300%; thus, it's advisable to swiftly kill the mob.

Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])
Mob Level: 592
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 90%

Drops: 8–16x [[Enchanted Clay Ball]] 8–16x Enchanted Jungle Wood ([[Enchanted Jungle Log]]) 0–1x [[Bobbin' Scriptures]] 0–1x [[Enchanted Clay Block]] 0–1x [[Tiki Mask]] 0–1x [[Troubled Bubble]] 0–1x [[Aquamarine Dye]]
XP: +14,800 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])

[Metadata]
ID: WIKI_TIKI_400; WIKI_TIKI_LASER_TOTEM_100
The Wiki Tiki is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspots]] in water.
## Spawning
The Wiki Tiki can be fished up from any [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] in water. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XL (40)]] or higher to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Hotspot Tonic Mixin]]|+10%|
|[[Hotspot Hook]]|+20%|
|[[Hotspot Bait]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Hermit Crab Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Wiki Tiki is 25, resulting in a catch chance of 0.25% (1 in 400 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] pool with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|The water bubbles and froths. A massive form emerges- you have disturbed the Wiki Tiki! You shall pay the price.|
## Behavior
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
The Wiki Tiki moves towards players and sprays a ring of damaging fire particles from the top head.
Periodically, the Wiki Tiki summons [[Wiki Tiki|Wiki Tiki Laser Totems]] that are [[Shielded]] and have 5 [[Health]]. They spin in place with a laser that damages players for 8,000 [[Damage]]. While the laser totem is active, the Wiki Tiki cannot be damaged. Attempting to damage the Wiki Tiki in this state sends the following message in chat:
The Wiki Tiki is IMMUNE! Attack its laser totems!
Occasionally, the Wiki Tiki compacts. While compacting, [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) blocks around it temporarily convert into Lime, Yellow, Orange, and Red Terracotta. Players still within the area after it reaches Red will take damage.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv592]:

# Yeti
Categories: Sea Creatures, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Events, Season of Jerry, Fishing, Mobs

Yeti is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water in the [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Spawning
Yeti can be fished up from water in the [[Jerry's Workshop]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing level XXV (25)]] to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Yeti is 100, resulting in a default catch chance of 1% (1 in 100 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|What is this creature!?|
## Behavior
The Yeti rarely moves. It attacks only once every 10 seconds, throwing a damaging hunk of terrain at the player similar to the [[Yeti Sword]] ability.

## Trivia
- When a player kills the Yeti for the first time, they are awarded the "[[Achievements|Frozen Monster]]" Achievement.
- The Yeti's home may be an igloo.
  - The [[Pocket-sized Igloo]], an item used to upgrade the [[Baby Yeti Pet]] from [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], references the Yeti by stating "Though far too small to be habitable, it reminds a certain creature of home."
- The Yeti previously had an OptiFine cape of the transgender flag.
  - On November 1, 2020, Admin LeaPhant purchased an Optifine cape bearing the transgender flag for it.

# Cluck the Fish
Aliases: Cluck The Fish

Type: Fish Family ([[The Fish Family]])

Item ID: CLUCK_THE_FISH
Tooltip: Cluck cluck bkaawwk. Puk Puk Pukaaak!; SPECIAL
Cluck cluck bkawwk. Puk Puk Pukaaak!
Cluck the Fish is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[The Fish Family|Fish Family]] item obtained from the chicken on [[Gulliver]]'s lead.
## Obtaining
### Gulliver's Chicken
Cluck the Fish can be obtained from the chicken connected to [[Gulliver]]. It will lay an egg approximately every 13 minutes, with a 0.3% chance to instead drop a Cluck the Fish.
## Usage
Cluck the Fish can be shown once to [[Coral]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], which will make it [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] but grant the player +2 [[Respiration]] and [[Fishing]] XP, as well as [[SkyBlock Levels|+3 SkyBlock XP]] if it's within the first 15 unique [[The Fish Family|fish]] shown.
## Trivia
- This item was given to 7ThirtyVirus and 7FizFez's co-op from 12Jayavarmen after they received 1,000,000,000 duplicated coins. They were the first players to obtain the item.
- Some people wrongly believe that hitting the chicken enough times causes the fish to drop. This is due to them having been next to the chicken while it laid Cluck the Fish, so they mistakenly thought hitting the chicken was the cause.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Added Cluck the Fish.|

# Frosty the Snow Blaster
Aliases: Frosty The Snow Blaster, Snow Blaster, Snow blaster
Categories: Season of Jerry, Epic items, Weapons, Crafting ingredients, Confirmations needed, Events
Jerry's Workshop Exclusive Frosty the Snow Blaster is only obtainable while [[Jerry's Workshop]] is open.
[Frosty the Snow Blaster]
Type: Weapon ([[Weapons]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Frosty the Snow Cannon]]
Next →: [[Frosty the Howitzer]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Sherry]]
Buy: 75,000 [[Coins]]
Sell: 100 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SNOW_BLASTER
Tooltip: Holds and shoots Snowballs. When you pickup Snowballs, they increase the ammo held by this item. Blasts Snowballs at a higher velocity than a Snow Cannon!; Snowballs: 0\ 2,000; Right click to shoot!; EPIC
Frosty the Snow Blaster is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item that can fire [[Snowball|Snowballs]] for the [[Season of Jerry]] event. It can be obtained from [[Sherry]].
## Obtaining
Frosty the Snow Blaster can be purchased for 75,000 [[Coins]] by interacting with [[Sherry]]. It is initially empty.
## Usage
The player can click or hold down right-click to shoot [[Snowball|Snowballs]]. The [[Snowball|Snowballs]] move at a faster rate than those shot by the [[Frosty the Snow Cannon|Cannon]].
Frosty the Snow Blaster has a capacity of 2,000 (31 stacks plus 16) [[Snowball|Snowballs]]. The ammo count is shown in the description and can be shown by left clicking while holding Frosty the Snow Blaster. The player can refill their ammo by interacting with [[Frosty]] for 1 [[Coins|Coin]] per [[Snowball]] missing. If the player does not meet the [[Coins]] requirement, [[Frosty]] will refill Frosty the Blaster based on the amount of [[Coins]] the player has.
If the player wears [[Snow Suit Armor|Snow Suit]] or [[Nutcracker Armor]], Frosty the Snow Blaster gains infinite ammo.
A [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Snowman Pet]] has a 50% chance to double damage done by [[Snowball|Snowballs]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Frosty the Howitzer]]|None|1x [[Fragmented Cryopowder]] 1x [[True Ice]] [[Frosty the Snow Blaster]] 1x Frosty the Snow Blaster 48x [[Walnut]]|Recipe: [[Walnut]] x8 ×6 + [[Fragmented Cryopowder]] + Frosty the Snow Blaster + [[True Ice]] → [[Frosty the Howitzer]]|
## Trivia
- Trying to place Frosty the Snow Blaster to put out a fire, successfully places the item. Breaking it returns it while removing all [[Snowball|Snowballs]].[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- [[Snowball|Snowballs]] fired from it can damage [[Grinch|Grinches]].

|Dec 19, 2019|Changed Frosty the Snow Blaster texture.|
|Dec 19, 2019|Removed Frosty the Snow Blaster UI.|

Frosty the Snow Cannon is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can fire [[Snowball|Snowballs]] for the [[Season of Jerry]] event. It can be obtained once from [[Frosty]] for free.
## Obtaining
Frosty the Snow Cannon can be obtained by talking to [[Frosty]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]]. More can be purchased for 1,000 [[Coins]] by interacting with [[Frosty]] without the original item in the player's inventory, or by using the command /opencannonbuymenu. It is initially completely filled with 1,000 (15 stacks plus 40) [[Snowball|Snowballs]].
## Usage
The player can click or hold down right-click to shoot [[Snowball|Snowballs]].
Frosty the Snow Cannon has a capacity of 1,000 [[Snowball|Snowballs]]. The ammo count is shown in the description and can be shown by left clicking while holding Frosty the Snow Cannon. The player can refill their ammo by interacting with [[Frosty]] for 1 [[Coins|Coin]] per [[Snowball]] missing. If the player does not meet the [[Coins]] requirement, [[Frosty]] will refill Frosty the Cannon based on the amount of [[Coins]] the player has.
If the player wears [[Snow Suit Armor|Snow Suit]] or [[Nutcracker Armor]], Frosty the Snow Cannon gains infinite ammo.
A [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Snowman Pet]] has a 50% chance to double damage done by [[Snowball|Snowballs]].
## Trivia
- Trying to place Frosty the Snow Cannon to put out a fire, successfully places the item. Breaking it returns it while removing all [[Snowball|Snowballs]].[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- [[Snowball|Snowballs]] fired from it can damage [[Grinch|Grinches]].

[Item Metadata]

The Frozen Chicken is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It gives +1 [[Speed]] when held in the player's [[Accessory Bag]].
## Obtaining
The Frozen Chicken is obtained from [[Gulliver]] after completing the third Cluckpoint of the [[Chicken Race]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]].
In case the player loses it, the Frozen Chicken can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Frozen Chicken [[Speed]]: +1
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Fried Frozen Chicken]]|None|[[Frozen Chicken]] 1x Frozen Chicken 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] 128x [[Enchanted Glacite]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Fried Feather]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Glacite]] x64 ×2 + [[Fried Feather]] x64 ×4 + [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] x64 ×2 + Frozen Chicken → [[Fried Frozen Chicken]]|
## Trivia
- The Accessory gives the same amount of speed as the [[Speed Talisman]] without any upgrade, unlocked in the [[Sugar Cane|Sugar Cane II (2)]] collection.
- Like all Accessories earned from [[Island Racing|Races]], with the exception of the [[Lynx Talisman]] and [[Cheetah Talisman]], it gives only +1 [[Speed]].

# Gift Compass
Categories: Season of Jerry, Uncommon items, Items, Events
Jerry's Workshop Exclusive Gift Compass is only obtainable while [[Jerry's Workshop]] is open.
[Gift Compass]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GIFT_COMPASS
Tooltip: When held, points towards the nearest unopened present while in Jerry's Workshop.; * Co-op Soulbound * UNCOMMON
Gift Compass is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item used in [[Jerry's Workshop]] to find unfound [[White Gift|White Gifts]].
## Obtaining
### Chicken Race
A Gift Compass can be obtained as a reward from the [[Chicken Race]] by reaching Cluckpoint 2.
### Shop Purchase
After being obtained from the [[Chicken Race]], the Gift Compass can be purchased from [[Rusty]] for 1,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
The Gift Compass points towards the nearest [[Gifts|Gift]] that has not been found on [[Jerry's Workshop]]. If the player has already found the gift it points to, they must hold it again in order to get the compass to point in the direction, but only if they are not directly holding it. Otherwise, it automatically resets. This process can repeat multiple times until the compass finds a gift that has not already been found.
When holding the compass after finding the gift it points to, it shows a message in the chat, saying either that all the gifts have been found or that the compass is pointing in the direction of a new gift.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Added Gift Compass.|
|Apr 25, 2022|Gift Compass was added to [[Rusty]]'s shop and is now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|

# Jingle Bells
Categories: Season of Jerry, Rare items, Items, Events, Support Items
Jerry's Workshop Exclusive Jingle Bells is only obtainable while [[Jerry's Workshop]] is open.
[Jingle Bells]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Source: 4th cluckpoint of the [[Chicken Race]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No

Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Shop]
Sell: 100 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: JINGLE_BELLS
Tooltip: Ability: Jingle Bells RIGHT CLICK Angers all monsters in a 10 block range, consuming 50% of your max mana and causing them to run towards you.; Cooldown: 1s; * Co-op Soulbound * RARE
Jingle Bells is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Support Items|Support item]] that angers nearby mobs.
## Obtaining
Jingle Bells can be obtained by beating the 4th cluckpoint in the [[Chicken Race]]. Once unlocked, it can be purchased from [[Rusty]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Uses
Item:
Jingle Bells
[Effects]
[Ability: Jingle Bells RIGHT CLICK; Angers all monsters in a 10 block range, consuming 50% of your total mana and causing them to run towards you.; Cooldown: 1s]
Jingle Bells best use is to redirect mobs to the user if their teammates are overwhelmed with mobs, buying time for them to heal. It is also a must item for [[Tank|tanks]] at higher Catacomb Floors.
A good use is at [[Thorn|Thorn Boss Fight]], to lure the [[Thorn|spirits animals]] into the fire circle. It can also be used in [[Maxor]], [[Storm]] and [[Necron]] Boss fight to take their aggro in order to lead Maxor to the laser easier, increase the fault tolerance during the process to crush Storm and control Necron's rush direction respectively.
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Apr 25, 2022|Jingle Bells was added to [[Rusty]]'s shop and is now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|

# White Gift Talisman
Aliases: Gift Talisman
Categories: Season of Jerry, Common items, Accessories, Events, Talisman
Jerry's Workshop Exclusive White Gift Talisman is only obtainable while [[Jerry's Workshop]] is open.
[White Gift Talisman]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Einary]]
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Green Gift Talisman]]
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Einary]]
Buy: 20 [[North Stars]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WHITE_GIFT_TALISMAN
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Gain +5% more Coins and Skill XP from gifts.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
White Gift Talisman is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that grants the [[Player]] +5% [[Coins|Coin]] and [[Skills|EXP]] rewards from [[Gifts]].
## Obtaining
### Purchase
The White Gift Talisman can be purchased from [[Einary]] at [[Einary's Emporium]] in [[Jerry's Workshop]].
Note: This item is purchasable only during December of real life. When attempting to buy it outside the period, the player will get a "Only sold in December!" error message. This applies to all upgrade purchases too.
|Merchant|Price|
|-|-|
|Merchant|[[Einary]] 20 [[North Stars]]|
### Raffle of the Century
White Gift Talisman can also be obtained as a MINOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]], or from the [[Gift Bag of the Century]].
## Usage
Accessory:
White Gift Talisman
[Effects]
[Grants +5% Coin and EXP rewards from gifts.]
### Upgrading
It can be used to purchase its upgraded form, the [[Green Gift Talisman]] from [[Einary]] for 1 White Gift Talisman and 40 [[North Stars]].
|Merchant|Price|
|-|-|
|Merchant|[[Einary]] [[White Gift Talisman]] 1x White Gift Talisman 40 [[North Stars]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 30, 2022|Added White Gift Talisman.|

# Agatha
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Agatha]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: David's Hunting Lessons ([[Quests]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Hosts the Starlyn Contests ([[Starlyn Contest]]) and sells various [[Foraging]]-related items.
Location: [[Murkwater Loch]]
Coordinates: -648.5 89 -3.5
Agatha is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Murkwater Loch]] on [[Moonglade Marsh]] that hosts the island's [[Starlyn Contest|Starlyn Contests]].
## Contests
Agatha hosts a contest that takes place every SkyBlock Day and lasts for 20 real-life minutes. During the contest, a player can gain points through various means.
## Shop
Agatha's Shop has 5 milestones, which are progressed by collecting [[Agatha's Coupon|Agatha's Coupons]]. Each contest awards a certain amount of [[Agatha's Coupon|Agatha's Coupons]] based on rarity.

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Agatha: Hello, aspiring forager! [NPC] Agatha: I'm Agatha, the youngest of the Starlyn Sisters. [NPC] Agatha: We each host contests on our own islands. [NPC] Agatha: Here in Moonglade Marsh, I'll reward your daily activities each SkyBlock day. [NPC] Agatha: So stick around, and please, avoid wandering into my sisters' territories.|
|Attempting to cut a Mangrove Tree with less than 50 [[Sweep]]|[NPC] Agatha: Hey! You should probably stick to Fig Trees! [NPC] Agatha: Mangrove Trees are too tough for you yet, try getting at least 50∮ Sweep!|
|Before unlocking [[Heart of the Forest]]|[NPC] Agatha: <player>! Have you talked to Hina at the front of the island to get your hands on a Heart of the Forest?|
|Talking before having talked to [[Swoop]]|[NPC] Agatha: I talked to Swoop the other day, he explained a lot of interesting Foraging Mechanics to me. [NPC] Agatha: Perhaps you should talk to him too, he's at the entrance of the village!|
|Talking before having talked to [[David Hunterborough]]|[NPC] Agatha: Hunting is incredible! You should talk to David Hunterborough in the Village to learn about it!|
|Talking before having talked to [[Kysha]]|[NPC] Agatha: Have you talked to Kysha, she knows all about Fusions!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Agatha: ✆ Hello again! [NPC] Agatha: ✆ You've been doing well in my contests recently, don't think I haven't noticed! [NPC] Agatha: ✆ Oh, my Abiphone Contact? [NPC] Agatha: ✆ Sure, I have some space. ✆ Agatha has been added to your Abiphone's contacts! [NPC] Agatha: ✆ I'll give you a call whenever you finish an Agatha's Contest so that you can claim your rewards!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Picking up Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]) call|[NPC] Agatha: ✆ Hey, the results for my latest contest are in! You reached the <rarity> bracket with # points!|
## Notes
- Agatha will only keep your rewards from previous contests for one Skyblock year.
## Trivia
- It costs to max out [[Fig Log|Fig]] Fortune, and for [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove]] Fortune.
  - This comes out to a total of if a player wishes to max out both.
- To reach Milestone V, it would take 100 [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] placement in the [[Starlyn Contest]], which will take 33 hours.
  - With [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], [[Rarity|DIVINE]] and [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] placements, it would take 29, 25 and 22 hours, respectively.
- Collecting Agatha's [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]] before unlocking her contests allows the player to call her and open the menu.
## See Also
- [[Miria]], Agatha's sister, and the second of the Starlyn Sisters.

# Alabaster
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters
[Alabaster]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells a tip for who the [[Stability Elixir]] supplier is.
Location: [[Shifted Tavern]]
Coordinates: -130.31 73.375 166.5
Alabaster is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Shifted Tavern]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Buying [[Shifted]]'s Tip|[NPC] Alabaster: I don't know who the supplier is, but I know someone who does. [NPC] Alabaster: I can tell you their name for 20 Motes. Select an option: [Buy tip]|
|Selecting [Buy tip]|[NPC] Alabaster: I heard that Dackinoru might know who the supplier is.|
## Trivia
- Alabaster has no dialogue unless having purchased [[Shifted]]'s tip. After talking to them, they will no longer give dialogue.
- Alabaster does, however, display text above their head, as if communicating with the other [[NPC|NPCs]] in the [[Shifted Tavern]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Alabaster.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

# Alchemist (Rift)
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Alchemist]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Gives out the [[Anti-Morph Potion]] in exchange for various items.
Location: [[West Village]]
Coordinates: -49.5 70 -65.5
The Alchemist is an [[NPC]] located in the [[West Village]]. After speaking to [[Detransfigured Seraphine]], they give out the [[Anti-Morph Potion]] in exchange for various items.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before talking to [[Detransfigured Seraphine]]|[NPC] Alchemist: Go away I'm busy. Select an option: [That's not very polite]|
|Selecting [That's not very polite]|[NPC] Alchemist: DID I STUTTER?|
|Without selecting [That's not very polite]|[NPC] Alchemist: ...|
|After speaking to [[Detransfigured Seraphine]]|[NPC] Alchemist: There is a darkness in you, [player]. Select an option: [No. There is a darkness in Seraphine]|
|Selecting [No. There is a darkness in Seraphine]|[NPC] Alchemist: Ugh... [NPC] Alchemist: Don't remind me! [NPC] Alchemist: Just thinking of that face makes me queasy. Select an option: [Barry said you could help]|
|Selecting [Barry said you could help]|[NPC] Alchemist: Yes of course. [NPC] Alchemist: I can help you prepare an Anti-Morph Potion, which should at least help with the situation. Select an option: [What is the first step?]|
|Selecting [What is the first step?]|[NPC] Alchemist: The first step is that you pay me 10,000 Motes! [NPC] Alchemist: An unhinged clown once told me, if you're good at something, never do it for free! [PAY MOTES]|
|Before paying the [[Motes]]|[NPC] Alchemist: I can help you prepare the Anti-Morph Potion to fix Seraphine's face. [NPC] Alchemist: But first I need 10,000 Motes. [PAY MOTES]|
|Selecting [PAY MOTES] (without enough)|You don't have enough Motes!|
|Selecting [PAY MOTES] (with enough), or before filling the cauldron|[NPC] Alchemist: Alrighty! [NPC] Alchemist: First, you need to fill the cauldron with water. [NPC] Alchemist: I can't help you with this, I'm counting my Motes.|
|Filling the cauldron with the [[Plumber's Bucket]], or before adding 64 (1 stack) [[Riftwart Roots]]|[NPC] Alchemist: Next up you should add 64 Riftwart Roots. [NPC] Alchemist: I've planted them around the building.|
|Holding [[Riftwart Roots]] when they're required|[NPC] Alchemist: Nice! Put it in the cauldron!|
|Inserting 64 [[Riftwart Roots]], or before adding 8 [[Larva Silk]]|[NPC] Alchemist: Now the Brew needs 8 Larva Silk. [NPC] Alchemist: It helps with the texture of the potion. [NPC] Alchemist: You can collect some back in the Wyld Woods.|
|Holding [[Larva Silk]] when they're required|[NPC] Alchemist: Nice! Put it in the cauldron!|
|Inserting 8 [[Larva Silk]], or before adding 4 [[Caducous Extract]]|[NPC] Alchemist: Finally you should add 4 Caducous Extract. [NPC] Alchemist: You can find them in the Dreadfarm. [NPC] Alchemist: It's needed for taste, otherwise we'll just get more vomit!|
|Holding [[Caducous Extract]] when they're required|[NPC] Alchemist: Nice! Put it in the cauldron!|
|Inserting 4 [[Caducous Extract]], or before transfiguring Seraphine ([[Detransfigured Seraphine]])|[NPC] Alchemist: Very good [player]! [NPC] Alchemist: With this Anti-Morph Potion... [NPC] Alchemist: Seraphine gets to look like something again. [NPC] Alchemist: I get to drink my favorite beverage. [NPC] Alchemist: And the world keeps spinning! [NPC] Alchemist: ... [NPC] Alchemist: Or not. [NPC] Alchemist: Anyway, splash this potion on whoever makes you want to vomit.|
|After transfiguring Seraphine ([[Detransfigured Seraphine]])|[NPC] Alchemist: Need another potion? [NPC] Alchemist: To be frank, I can see why you could need another one.|

# Alda

Alda is an [[NPC]] located in [[Abiphones & Co.]]. They introduce the player to [[Abiphones]] and sell the [[Abiphones|Abiphone Basic]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Alda: Welcome to Abiphones & Co., <Username>! [NPC] Alda: Are you here to buy an Abiphone? Select an option: [What's an Abiphone?]|
|Selecting [What's an Abiphone?]|[NPC] Alda: Have ya ever needed to talk to or buy something from an NPC, but they're on a different island? [NPC] Alda: And getting to them would take ages, even if you had a Travel Scroll? [NPC] Alda: That's where the Abiphone comes in! [NPC] Alda: Simply add an NPC as a Contact and you can call them anytime, anywhere! [NPC] Alda: Sounds amazing, right? Select an option: [I guess.]|
|Selecting [I guess.]|[NPC] Alda: Then why don't ya buy one? I'll do a little discount, just for you.|
|After buying an Abiphone Basic ([[Abiphones]])|[NPC] Alda: Congratulations on becoming a proud owner of an Abiphone! [NPC] Alda: Before you go, let me show you how to add a contact. [NPC] Alda: Simply hold the Abiphone in your hand, and click on an NPC! [NPC] Alda: Why don't you try it out on me now?|
|Abiphone Contact|[NPC] Alda: ✆ Nice job! [NPC] Alda: ✆ It's really as simple as that. I'll add myself to your Contacts now! [NPC] Alda: ✆ One last thing - not every NPC owns an Abiphone, so you can't add everyone as a Contact. [NPC] Alda: ✆ To see who you can add, simply open up your phone and check the Contacts Directory. [NPC] Alda: ✆ That's it. Enjoy your purchase!|

# Anita
Aliases: Extra Farming Drops, Extra Farming Fortune
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Farming
[Anita]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
[Locations]
Description: Sells various [[Farming]] items in exchange for Jacob's Tickets ([[Jacob's Ticket]]) and [[Medals]].
Location: [[Farmhouse]]
Coordinates: 53.5 73 -128.5
Location: [[The Garden]]
Anita is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Farmhouse]] and in the player's [[The Garden]]. She sells unique [[Farming]] related items and buffs in exchange for [[Jacob's Ticket|Jacob's Tickets]] and [[Medals]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Anita: Hey hey hey! [NPC] Anita: I'll trade your Jacob's Tickets for farming tools!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Anita: ✆ Hi dear! [NPC] Anita: ✆ I only share my contact with those who are on Jacob's contact list. [NPC] Anita: ✆ Oh and also, you need to have earned a GOLD medal in his contest if you want to be on MY list!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Anita: ✆ Sweet! [NPC] Anita: ✆ If I need any farming done back home, I'll be sure to call ya!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Anita: Hey hey hey again! I'll trade you some copper for some carrots!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Anita: Yay, thank you so much!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Anita: Oh, darn it :(|
## Upgrades
### Personal Bests
Personal Bests are a purchasable perk within Anita's shop. Once purchased, the amount of [[Crops (disambiguation)|Crops]] collected during [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]] by the player will be tallied, and based on their high score, they will receive an appropriate [[Crop Fortune]] increase. The value is capped at 100 .
|Crop|Crops collected per 0.1 [[Crop Fortune]]|Crops collected for 100 [[Crop Fortune]]|
|-|-|-|
|[[Wheat]]|1,000|1,000,000|
|[[Carrot]]|3,000|3,000,000|
|[[Potato]]|3,000|3,000,000|
|[[Pumpkin]]|1,000|1,000,000|
|[[Melon Slice]]|5,000|5,000,000|
|[[Mushroom]]|1,000|1,000,000|
|[[Cactus]]|2,000|2,000,000|
|[[Sugar Cane]]|2,000|2,000,000|
|[[Nether Wart]]|3,000|3,000,000|
|[[Cocoa Beans]]|3,000|3,000,000|
|[[Sunflower]]|2,000|2,000,000|
|[[Moonflower]]|2,000|2,000,000|
|[[Wild Rose]]|2,000|2,000,000|
### Extra Farming Fortune
Extra Farming Fortune is a purchasable perk in Anita's shop. It can be purchased multiple times to increase your [[Farming Fortune]], up to +60 [[Farming Fortune]].
|[[Farming Fortune]]|Costs per Tier Gold Medal ([[Medals]]) Cost|Costs per Tier [[Jacob's Ticket]] Cost|Cumulative Costs Gold Medal ([[Medals]]) Cost|Cumulative Costs [[Jacob's Ticket]] Cost|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|+4 [[Farming Fortune]]|1 Gold Medal ([[Medals]])||1 Gold Medal ([[Medals]])||
|+8 [[Farming Fortune]]|1 Gold Medal ([[Medals]])|50 [[Jacob's Ticket]]|2 Gold Medal ([[Medals]])|50 [[Jacob's Ticket]]|
|+12 [[Farming Fortune]]|1 Gold Medal ([[Medals]])|50 [[Jacob's Ticket]]|3 Gold Medal ([[Medals]])|100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Jacob's Ticket]]|

## Tips
- For maximising the Personal Best perk, players can spawn 8 [[Pests]] of the respective crop and [[Vacuums|Vacuum]] them at the start of the [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Farming Contest]], as well as plant the crops in [[Greenhouse]] and harvest them during the event. Using this, it's simple to reach the highest required personal best and beyond.

Archie is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Critter Safari Entrance]] on the [[Torrhus Canyon]] and the [[Critter Safari]]. They introduce the player to [[Safari Essence]] and the Safari Essence Shop.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Archie: Hello and welcome to the Critter Safari! [NPC] Archie: Say, have you heard of Safari Essence? Select an option: [Yes] [No]|
|Selecting [Yes]|[NPC] Archie: Then there's no need for me to explain! [NPC] Archie: All of the Safari Essence you gain while in the Critter Safari can be spent in my shop. [NPC] Archie: You can use it to buy useful perks that will help you out while in the zone. [NPC] Archie: Here, take a look!|
|Selecting [No]|[NPC] Archie: Then allow me to explain! [NPC] Archie: Safari Essence can only be gained while in the Critter Safari. There are two ways of obtaining it. [NPC] Archie: Firstly, it can be obtained from hunting mobs in the Critter Safari. [NPC] Archie: The rarer a mob's shard, the more Safari Essence it will drop when hunted. [NPC] Archie: Secondly, you can sometimes find Safari Essence on the ground while walking around in the Critter Safari. [NPC] Archie: So, if you ever see something shining on the ground. be sure to pick it up! [NPC] Archie: All of the Safari Essence you gain while in the Critter Safari can be spent in my shop. [NPC] Archie: You can use it to buy useful perks that will help you out while in the zone. [NPC] Archie: Here, take a look!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Archie: ✆ Critter Safari been treating you well? [NPC] Archie: ✆ Hmm, you want my Abiphone Contact? [NPC] Archie: ✆ Sure, I suppose, why not? [NPC] Archie: ✆ Not much else to do here on slow days except get sunburn. [NPC] Archie: ✆ Bring me a Helixis and I'm sure we can strike a deal. [GIVE ITEM]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [GIVE ITEM] without [[Helixis]]|[NPC] Archie: ✆ Err, buddy, I don't see a Helixis on you. [NPC] Archie: ✆ You sure you had one? Did you lose it, maybe?|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [GIVE ITEM] with [[Helixis]]|[NPC] Archie: ✆ Cheers man! That's exactly what I was after. [NPC] Archie: ✆ Woodworking is my true passion, you see, but I'm stuck behind this desk all day. [NPC] Archie: ✆ And then, when I'm finally done, I haven't got any energy left to pursue my passions. [NPC] Archie: ✆ Vicious cycle, huh? [NPC] Archie: ✆ ... [NPC] Archie: ✆ Anyways... Sorry about that. [NPC] Archie: ✆ Here's my Abiphone Contact!|

## Salvaging
Archie also allows the player to salvage [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] obtained from the [[Critter Safari]] for [[Safari Essence]]. The amount of [[Safari Essence]] the player receives is dependent on its rarity.
|Rarity|Amount|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|1 [[Safari Essence]]|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|2 [[Safari Essence]]|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|4 [[Safari Essence]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|8 [[Safari Essence]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|16 [[Safari Essence]]|

The Argofay Threebrothers are a group of three [[NPC|NPCs]] located in the [[Wyld Woods]]. Each of them appears in one of the three trees there and provides dialogue. After speaking to all three, each of them will offer to sell the player a [[Wyld Sword]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Brother First interaction|[NPC] Argofay Threebrother: It's rare to meet new faces around these woods. [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: Especially one who is so ill-prepared! [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: I don't have it on me, but one of my two brothers should have a spare weapon. [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: If you run into them, tell them I sent you. [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: They will know what it means.|
|Second Brother First interaction|[NPC] Argofay Threebrother: It's rare to meet new faces around these woods. Select an option: [Your brother sent me.]|
|Second Brother Selecting [Your brother sent me.]|[NPC] Argofay Threebrother: That is suspicious. [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: He wouldn't let you leave so ill-prepared. [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: My other brother should have a spare weapon. [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: Whatever you do, do not call him by his name, that might trigger him to use it on you. Select an option: [I don't know his name.]|
|Second Brother Selecting [I don't know his name.]|[NPC] Argofay Threebrother: Perfect.|
|Third Brother First interaction|[NPC] Argofay Threebrother: It's rare to meet new faces around these woods. Select an option: [I am ill-prepared.]|
|Third Brother Selecting [I am ill-prepared.]|[NPC] Argofay Threebrother: What a strange thing to say. [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: Did you talk with my brothers? [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: Anyway, you're right... [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: But I do have a weapon that could help you!|
|None; Having spoken to one brother|[NPC] Argofay Threebrother: Try and find one of my brothers. [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: They should be able to equip you with a weapon.|
|None; Having spoken to two brothers|[NPC] Argofay Threebrother: Try and find one of my brothers. [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: They keep a spare weapon around for new faces.|
|None; Having spoken to three brothers; without owning a [[Wyld Sword]]|[NPC] Argofay Threebrother: I have a spare weapon. [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: Here.|
|None; Having spoken to three brothers; while owning a [[Wyld Sword]]|[NPC] Argofay Threebrother: A spare weapon? [NPC] Argofay Threebrother: Are you sure you're not mistaking me with someone else?|
## Trivia
- "Argofay" is likely Pig Latin for "Fargo".

The Auction Agents are [[NPC|NPCs]] located in the [[Auction House]].
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Usage
Right-clicking on any Auction Agent will open the [[Auction House]] menus.
## Trivia
- Trying to get too close to any of the Auction Agents (or the [[Auction Master]]) will get the player pushed away. Doing this a few times will teleport you outside the [[Auction House]].
- In the 0.24.2 update, the size of the physical [[Auction House]] was reduced, with which the number of Auction Agents dropped.

# Baker
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Events, New Year Celebration
[Baker]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Appears during the [[New Year Celebration]] to give away New Year Cakes ([[New Year Cake]]) and sell [[New Year Cake Bag]].
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: -37.5 69 16.5
The Baker is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Village]]. They give away [[New Year Cake|New Year Cakes]] during the [[New Year Celebration]].
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
Note: The year appended to the cakes lore will be specific to the year in which it was claimed.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Baker: At the end of each year I bake cakes for everyone in town to celebrate the year. [NPC] Baker: I made one especially for you, here you go. [NPC] Baker: Wondering where to store your cakes? You can buy a New Year Cake Bag from me. Click me again to open my shop!|
|[[The Garden]] First Interaction|[NPC] Baker: My Wheat Minions produce the finest flour around, but they can't keep up with today's demand...|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Baker: That should do it! Thanks for your help, <player>.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Baker: "Use better minion fuel"? But... what could possibly be better than Enchanted Bread?.|
## Trivia
- During the first [[Jerry's Workshop]] event, the Baker stood in the same spot as Jerry did in the event area.
- If the player opens the Baker's interface and the Baker leaves, the menu remains open and can still be interacted with.
- The Baker awards the most [[Copper]] of any [[The Garden|Garden Visitor]] at 550 [[Copper]], or 625 [[Copper]] with the Ladybug attribute.
- The third dialogue line of the Baker used to say "I've recently added a New Year Cake Bag to my inventory. Sadly, it's not free! Click me again to open my shop!"

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Added Happy New Year Achievement ([[Achievements]]) for obtaining cake from the Baker.|

|Sep 8, 2022|Removed delay before Baker begins talking.|

# Bartender
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Bartender]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Time to Strike ([[Quests]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
[Metadata]
ID: BARTENDER
[Locations]
Description: Gives the player an [[Undead Sword]] and tasks them with killing 10 zombies.
Location: [[Combat Settlement]]
Coordinates: -40.5 69 -72.5
Description: Sells various [[Brews]] and [[Mixins]], which can be used to upgrade [[Potions]].
Location: [[Tavern]]
Coordinates: -83.5 74 -135.5
The Bartender is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Combat Settlement]] and [[Tavern]]. They give the player an [[Undead Sword]] and task them with killing 10 [[Zombie|Zombies]] or [[Graveyard Zombie|Graveyard Zombies]]. After killing the Zombies, the player must travel to the [[Spider's Den]]. The Bartender will then open their shop to the player.
## Quest
The Bartender is the first [[NPC]] that the player is directed to speaking to as a [[Quests|Quest]] after joining the [[Hub Island]]. Upon speaking to them, they give the player an [[Undead Sword]], then task them with killing 10 [[Zombie|Zombies]] or [[Graveyard Zombie|Graveyard Zombies]]. Doing so grants the player [[SkyBlock Levels|+5 SkyBlock XP]] and +100 [[Combat|Combat XP]]. After speaking to the Bartender again, the player earns 100 [[Coins]], and after switching servers, they then move to the [[Tavern]] to sell items.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Bartender: Ah! You look like someone strong. [NPC] Bartender: These are trying times, indeed. The Graveyard up ahead is overflowing with monsters! Anyone who comes in is spooked off by the grunts of Zombies in the distance. [NPC] Bartender: Could you give me a hand? If you help clear out some of these monsters, I'll pay you for it!|
|After killing 10 Zombies ([[Zombie]]) or Graveyard Zombies ([[Graveyard Zombie]])|[NPC] Bartender: Words cannot describe how thankful I am, [player]. [NPC] Bartender: If you're up for the challenge, both the Graveyard and the Spider's Den beyond it are great training grounds for improving your Combat Skill. [NPC] Bartender: For now, here's a reward for helping me out!|
|Before moving to the [[Tavern]] after the player switches servers|[NPC] Bartender: Be careful in the Graveyard during the night time, the Zombies are stronger and even more of them like to show up!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) while; below Spider Slayer 9 ([[Spider Slayer]])|[NPC] Bartender: ✆ Sorry mate, not much space in the old handheld. [NPC] Bartender: ✆ People always adding me left and right these days, asking if the ol' tav is open or not. [NPC] Bartender: ✆ I tell 'em every time that it's open every day, but when they put down the phone I know they'll call 'n ask again tomorrow. [NPC] Bartender: ✆ Since I'm so limited on space, I've got to be quite particular on who I add. [NPC] Bartender: ✆ You go and reach Spider Slayer 9, and I'll add you. How's that sound?|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) while Spider Slayer 9 ([[Spider Slayer]])|[NPC] Bartender: ✆ You want me to add ya to my handheld, huh? [NPC] Bartender: ✆ Looks like you've reached Spider Slayer 9, so you're as good a choice as any I suppose. [NPC] Bartender: ✆ Just don't call me asking when the tavern is open, okay? [NPC] Bartender: ✆ We're always open...|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Bartender: I want to make some new brews, do you think you can give me some ingredients to work with?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Bartender: Many Thanks! I'll be sure to let you have the first taste as soon as it's done... at a discounted price of course.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Bartender: Hard times we are in. Take care!|

# Bingo (NPC)
Aliases: Bingo Shop
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Bingo]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Allows the player to create a [[Bingo]] profile or access the Bingo Shop ([[Bingo (NPC)]]).
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: 3.5 78 4.5
Bingo is an NPC located in front of the [[Community Center]] in the Hub. The NPC hosts a SkyBlock Bingo for the first seven days of every real life month. The Bingo Shop can be accessed through Bingo.
## Bingo Shop
Bingo sells the following items for [[Bingo|Bingo Points]].
|Slot|Item|Price|Requirements|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bingo Display]]|[[Bingo Display]]|50 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])|Ⓑ No Bingo Rank|
|[[Collection Display]]|[[Collection Display]]|30 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])|Ⓑ No Bingo Rank|
|[[Bonzo Statue]]|[[Bonzo Statue]]|30 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])|Ⓑ No Bingo Rank|
|[[Book of Stats]]|[[Book of Stats]]|5 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])|Ⓑ No Bingo Rank|
|[[Spring Boots]]|[[Spring Boots]]|150 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])|Ⓑ Bingo Rank I (1)|
|[[Bingo Talisman]]|[[Bingo Talisman]]|100 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])|Ⓑ Bingo Rank I (1)|
|[[Bingo Ring]]|[[Bingo Ring]]|150 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]]); 1 [[Bingo Talisman]]|Ⓑ Bingo Rank II (2)|
|[[Golden Dante Statue (Item)]]|Golden Dante Statue ([[Golden Dante Statue (Item)]])|100 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])|Ⓑ Bingo Rank II (2)|
|[[Ditto Skull]]|[[Ditto Skull]]|50 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])|Ⓑ Bingo Rank II (2)|
|[[Bingo Artifact]]|[[Bingo Artifact]]|150 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]]); 1 [[Bingo Ring]]|Ⓑ Bingo Rank III (3)|
|[[Bingo Blue Dye]]|[[Bingo Blue Dye]]|500 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])|Ⓑ Bingo Rank III (3)|
|[[Ditto Skin]]|[[Ditto Skin]]|100 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])|Ⓑ Bingo Rank III (3)|
|[[Bingo Relic]]|[[Bingo Relic]]|200 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]]); 1 [[Bingo Artifact]]|Ⓑ Bingo Rank IV (4)|
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before [[Bingo]]|[NPC] Bingo: It's time to play SkyBlock Bingo! [NPC] Bingo: If you join, you have 7 days to complete as many tasks as you can on a fresh, temporary profile! [NPC] Bingo: Complete personal tasks and work towards Community Goals to earn Bingo Points. [NPC] Bingo: At the end of the event, your Bingo profile will be deleted. [NPC] Bingo: But don't fret, all of the Bingo Points you earn during the event will stick around! [NPC] Bingo: You can spend these points in the Bingo Shop on any profile! [NPC] Bingo: Check back soon to join the <month> <year> Bingo Event!|
|During [[Bingo]]|[NPC] Bingo: Welcome to the <month> <year> SkyBlock Bingo event! [NPC] Bingo: You have 7 days to complete as many tasks as you can on a fresh, temporary profile! [NPC] Bingo: Complete personal tasks and work towards Community Goals to earn Bingo Points. [NPC] Bingo: At the end of the event, your Bingo profile will be deleted. [NPC] Bingo: But don't fret, all of the Bingo Points you earn during the event will stick around! [NPC] Bingo: You can spend these points in the Bingo Shop on any profile! [NPC] Bingo: Good luck this week. And remember...haaaaave fun with it!|
|After [[Bingo]]|[NPC] Bingo: The <month> <year> Bingo Event has concluded! [NPC] Bingo: I will stick around for a bit longer, but then I'll be gone until next event at the start of the month! [NPC] Bingo: Check out the Bingo Shop to spend Bingo Points while supplies last!|

Description: Allows the player to Reforge ([[Reforging]]) items.
Location: [[Dwarven Village]]
Coordinates: -6.5 201 -154.5
The Blacksmith is an [[NPC]] located in [[Blacksmith (Zone)|Blacksmith]], [[Gold Mine]], and [[Dwarven Village]]. They are a service character that will [[Reforging|Reforge]] your gear for a fee, depending on the [[Rarity]] of your gear.
## Reforging
There is an [[Anvil]] next to the blacksmith, allowing the player to [[Enchantments|Enchant]] their gear using books at the cost of experience levels.
## Quest
When first interacting with the Blacksmith, he introduces you to the service he provides and then gives the player a [[Quests|Quest]], titled "Time To Mine". This requires the player to mine 10 [[Coal]] and reach [[Mining|Mining I (1)]]. After doing so, the [[Player]] will unlock the Blacksmith's menu.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Blacksmith: I'm the town Blacksmith! I can reforge items for you, for a price. [NPC] Blacksmith: Reforging usually costs Coins, but since I'm feeling friendly I can reforge your first item for Coal x10. [NPC] Blacksmith: Go into the Mine to collect Coal, then come back to learn how to reforge items!|
|During the Mine Coal objective|[NPC] Blacksmith: Mine some more Coal in the Mine!|
|During the Mine Coal objective|[NPC] Blacksmith: I will teach you how to reforge items once you mine some more Coal!|
|Before reaching Mining I (1) ([[Mining]]) or finishing the Mine Coal objective|[NPC] Blacksmith: Reach Mining Level I to gain access to the Gold Mine, north of the Coal Mine. [NPC] Blacksmith: The Gold Mine is more dangerous than the Coal Mine, but it has better ores and treasures!|
|Before visiting the [[Gold Mine]] or finishing the Mine Coal objective|[NPC] Blacksmith: Travel to the Gold Mine to spelunk for better ores, including iron and gold!|
|First Interaction after completing the Mine Coal objective|[NPC] Blacksmith: Ahh, excellent! [NPC] Blacksmith: Reforging items allows you to get the most out of your weapons, armor, and other items by applying stat modifiers to them! [NPC] Blacksmith: To reforge an item, place an item in my inventory. Reforging costs Coins - the more prestigious items cost more to reforge! [NPC] Blacksmith: However, this time I will reforge any item for the low price of Coal x10!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Intereacting while holding Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]).|[NPC] Blacksmith: ✆ You want my contact? [NPC] Blacksmith: ✆ Nonono, not happening. [NPC] Blacksmith: ✆ Well... Actually... [NPC] Blacksmith: ✆ No, no absolutely not. [NPC] Blacksmith: ✆ Unless? Hmm, lemme think about it. [NPC] Blacksmith: ✆ No for sure, for sure. That would be too much. [NPC] Blacksmith: ✆ But maybe... If you do something for me. [NPC] Blacksmith: ✆ I owe a lot of money to someone... Someone important. [NPC] Blacksmith: ✆ Don't ask me how I got into this situation. [NPC] Blacksmith: ✆ I owe them 32M coins. [NPC] Blacksmith: ✆ If you help me, I'll allow you to use my services from anywhere. [PAY DEBT]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Paying Debt|[NPC] Blacksmith: ✆ You are a lifesaver. [NPC] Blacksmith: ✆ I swear I wont get in that trouble again! ✆ Blacksmith has been added to your Abiphone's contacts!|
## Trivia
- Despite the [[NPC]] having identical appearance and usage in the [[Gold Mine]] or the [[Dwarven Village]], only the one in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] has an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact.
  - This is because the [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] system requires a single [[NPC]], and there are 3 different Blacksmiths.

# Builder
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Blocks
[Builder]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Sells various blocks that can be used on the player's [[Private Island]].
Location: [[Builder's House]]
Coordinates: -8.5 71 -61.5
The Builder is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Builder's House]] within the [[Village]]. They sell a wide variety of decorative blocks.
## Mechanics
### Stranded
On the [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] game mode, players can unlock the Builder by unlocking every collection, with the exception of a few collections that are unobtainable on Stranded.
Once the requirement is met, the Builder is permanently unlocked, even if new collections are added to the game in the future.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Builder: If you build, they will come! [NPC] Builder: Click me again to open the Builder Shop!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Builder: ✆ Oh? [NPC] Builder: ✆ Yes... I could give you my contact... However, may I ask a favor in return? [NPC] Builder: ✆ For a builder to not have a Builder's Wand is quite uncommon. [NPC] Builder: ✆ If you give me one I'll gladly give you my contact! [GIVE ITEM]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving [[Builder's Wand]]|[NPC] Builder: ✆ A Builder's Wand! This is a dream come true!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Without [[Builder's Wand]]|[NPC] Builder: ✆ You don't have what I need! Nice try!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Before 0.19.9 Update|[NPC] Builder: It do be like that.|
## Trivia
- The Builder's appearance bears a striking resemblance to Flim and Flam from the My Little Pony series, which could indicate them being an inspiration.
- The Builder's dialogue, "If you build, they will come!", is likely a reference to the 1989 film Field of Dreams, in which the phrase "If you build it, he will come" is repeated.
- The list of collections which are unobtainable on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] used to be manually maintained, which has resulted in updates adding new collections preventing Stranded players from unlocking the Builder on several occasions. Eventually, the requirement was removed entirely.

# Croesus
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Dungeons
[Croesus]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Allows the player to purchase Dungeon Reward Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]) and Kuudra Reward Chests ([[Kuudra/Loot]]) from runs they've completed that haven't been purchased yet.
Location: [[Dungeon Hub]]
Coordinates: -27.5 119 35.5
Croesus is an [[NPC]] in the [[Dungeon Hub]]. They will store a player's [[Dungeon Reward Chest]] and [[Kuudra/Loot|Kuudra Reward Chest]] from any [[Dungeons|Floor]]. Croesus can keep up to 60 runs, and each run has a 72 hours expiry date. The cost of the chests still apply.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Croesus: After completed a dungeon run, you get a selection of treasure chests. [NPC] Croesus: If you don't open any, I collect and hoard them for you!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[NPC] Croesus: Did you know, I used to live in the catacombs before they moved in all the zombies?|
|None|[NPC] Croesus: It's always best to keep a handle on things!|
|None|[NPC] Croesus: The Wither King now lives in the deepest floor of the Catacombs, but where is the Wither Prince?|
|None|[NPC] Croesus: I wonder if they'll ever open more doors in here.|
## Trivia
- Croesus is a reference to the king of Lydia of the same name. He was renowned for his wealth, and his gifts were preserved after his death, similarly to Croesus.
- In their dialogue, Croesus makes a pun out of keeping a handle on things, referring to the [[Necron's Handle]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:

|Sep 28, 2022|0.15|Added Croesus.|

|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Croesus now gives loot directly instead of dropping it on the ground.|
|Oct 15, 2025|0.23.5|Moved Croesus and allowed him to store Kuudra Reward Chests.|

|First interaction|[NPC] Cryptosis: What's a BZT, you ask? [NPC] Cryptosis: Well everyone is talking about them, so you should know, bro! [NPC] Cryptosis: BZT is immutable digital ownership! # Detransfigured Seraphine
Aliases: Seraphine (Rift), Transfigured Seraphine
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters
[Detransfigured Seraphine]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Tasks the player with obtaining the [[Anti-Morph Potion]] to fix her face.
Location: [[Barry Center]]
Coordinates: -16.5 71 -101
Detransfigured Seraphine is an [[NPC]] in the [[Barry Center]].
## Quest
Detransfigured Seraphine appears in the [[Barry Center]] after the player resolves all of the protestors' issues. They ask the player to talk to the [[Alchemist (Rift)|Alchemist]], who will give the player an [[Anti-Morph Potion]] after the player gives them the following:
- 10,000 [[Motes]]
- 1 [[Plumber's Bucket]]
- 64 (1 stack) [[Riftwart Roots]]
- 8 [[Larva Silk]]
- 4 [[Caducous Extract]]
After the player uses an Anti-Morph Potion on Detransfigured Seraphine, she will turn into Transfigured Seraphine, and will allow the player to buy the [[Used Detransfigured Face]] for 1 [[Agaricus Cap Cap]] and 1,500 [[Motes]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before completing initial dialogue|[NPC] Seraphine: [player]! [NPC] Seraphine: It's me! [NPC] Seraphine: Clerk Seraphine! [NPC] Seraphine: I stepped into the Rift to help with the elections... [NPC] Seraphine: ... but something terrible happened to me! Select an option: [Are you okay?]|
|Selecting [Are you okay?]|[NPC] Seraphine: I feel fine, but I wish it could be fixed. [NPC] Seraphine: Barry told me that the Alchemist could help. [NPC] Seraphine: But every time I talk to him, he gets uncontrollable nausea and starts vomiting incessantly. [NPC] Seraphine: Somehow, anyone who looks at me starts vomiting! Select an option: [I'm starting to feel why.]|
|Selecting [I'm starting to feel why.]|[NPC] Seraphine: Oh? [NPC] Seraphine: Noooo! [NPC] Seraphine: Your face is turning green too! [NPC] Seraphine: PLEASE! [NPC] Seraphine: Go to the Alchemist and help me fix my face!|
|Before obtaining an [[Anti-Morph Potion]]|[NPC] Seraphine: Please go talk to the Alchemist. [NPC] Seraphine: Barry told me he could fix my face.|
|The player has an Anti-Morph Potion in their Inventory|[NPC] Seraphine: Did you get a potion from the Alchemist? [NPC] Seraphine: Quick! Splash it on me!|
|Splashing the Anti-Morph Potion on Detransfigured Seraphine|[NPC] Seraphine: Whew! [NPC] Seraphine: My face literally fell off! [NPC] Seraphine: Well, if I'm not making everyone throw up, it's a welcome improvement! :) [NPC] Seraphine: Thanks [player]! [NPC] Seraphine: Here, take my old face!|
|After using the Anti-Morph Potion|[NPC] Seraphine: Thank you so much for helping to fix my face, [player]! [NPC] Seraphine: Do you need my old one again?|
## Trivia
- Despite her dialogue saying she stepped into the Rift Dimension, she is still present in the regular Hub.
  - This may be because the Rift Dimension simultaneously has people from the past and future, so Seraphine (Rift) could hail from the future.
  - She might also be a version of Seraphine from another universe.
## History

|May 4, 2023|Added Seraphine.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Seraphine.|

# Diana
Aliases: Candidate Diana, Diana the Mythological Pet Caretaker, Mayor Diana, Minister Diana
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages containing missing tooltips, Pages with UIs, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election
[Diana Diana]
Also known as: Mayor Diana
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
[Locations]
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]), or nearby when elected as Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 6.5 79 19.5
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]). Also appears on an outer stand when running to become Mayor or Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Election Room]]
Coordinates: -3.5 49 34.5
Tooltip: Year # Candidate; Votes: # (#%); Last elected: #y ago; __________________________ Huntress' Intuition Gain +10❃ Tracking.; Mythological Ritual Mayor Diana will sell the Griffin pet, which lets you find Mythological Creatures and tons of unique items.; Pet XP Buff Gain 35% more pet XP.; Sharing is Caring Unlocks 2 more Exp Share slots in your pets menu and increases the Exp Share rate by +10%!; __________________________; ✯ Minister Perks are also granted if this mayor wins second place!; Click to vote for Diana!
Diana, the Mythological Pet Caretaker, is a mayoral candidate that can be elected during the [[Mayor Election]] event. Her perks focus around [[Pets]] and [[Mythological|Mythological Creature]] hunting. She sells the [[Griffin Pet]] for 25,000 [[Coins]], and the [[Ancestral Spade]] for 5,000 [[Coins]] and 32 [[Gold Ingot|Gold Ingots]].
## Mayoral Perks
Candidate Diana may run for election with up to 4 possible perks.
  [[Diana]] Diana the Mythological Pet Caretaker
1. Huntress' Intuition: Gain +10 [[Tracking]]
2. [[Mythological Ritual]]: Mayor Diana will sell the [[Griffin Pet]], which lets you find [[Mythological|Mythological Creatures]] and tons of unique items.
3. Pet XP Buff: Gain +35% more pet XP.
4. Sharing is Caring: You can have up to 3 EXP Shared Pets active at once. Your EXP Share rate is increased by 10%.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Dialogue
- The color of the NPC's name changes depending on which color they are in the current [[Mayor Election]]. Yellow has been used as a default color.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Diana: Pets are the best, trust me. [NPC] Diana: If I get elected I SWEAR you won't regret it.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Mayor Diana: My Griffin's getting reaaal hungry. I need to find some food QUICKLY!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Mayor Diana: I was worried he would take a nab at me!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Mayor Diana: If you ran for mayor, I would not vote for you.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Diana: My private island is a Zoo, you should come by and visit.|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Diana: Griffins are real, but they only like pure people.|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Diana: I am in perfect communication with nature.|
## Trivia
- Diana is most likely based on the Diana of Roman Mythology, who was generally regarded as the Goddess of the Hunt. The in-game items and mayoral events seem to reinforce this, as they are primarily related to Greek and Roman mythology.
- Diana used to be the only mayor that is also a shop until [[Dante]] and [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]] were elected.
- Diana was the first mayor elected in [[SkyBlock]], winning the Year 88 election with only the Mythological Ritual perk.
- Diana was also the first mayor to be elected with all 3 perks.
- Diana, [[Marina]], [[Cole]], and [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]] are the only mayors to give direct stats.
- All Pet XP boosts work multiplicatively together with [[Taming]], the [[Beastmaster Crest]], and [[Pet Items]].

Doug is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Carnival]] during the [[Carnival]] event. They have a shop menu where players can spend [[Carnival Tokens]] to obtain [[Carnival Masks]] and buffs to the [[Fishing Festival]], [[Mining Fiesta]], [[Mythological Ritual]], [[Spooky Festival]], [[Season of Jerry]], and [[Harvest Feast]] events.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Mask Shop
[[Carnival Masks]] can be purchased at Doug, which can be worn for minor but unique effects and stored in the [[Carnival Mask Bag]] for +1 [[Intelligence]].

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Doug: Masks. Event buffs. An Accessory. [NPC] Doug: You want them? [NPC] Doug: They're yours, my friend. [NPC] Doug: As long as you have enough Carnival Tokens.|
## Trivia
- Doug's Dialogue is a reference to the popular quotes from Morshu from the game Link: The Faces of Evil.
- Doug was named after Hypixel player SuperDuggy117, who wrote lots of dialogue for the Better Mayors update, including this one.
- As of 0.27, 27,050 [[Carnival Tokens]] are needed to purchase all items and perks from [[Doug]] Doug :
  - Masks Shop requires 7,250 [[Carnival Tokens]]. If solely [[Chantelle|Carnival Goals]] were used to gain [[Carnival Tokens]], it would take 6 [[Carnival|Carnivals]] to purchase all its items.
  - Each of the Event Shops requires 3,300 [[Carnival Tokens]] to max, resulting in a total of 19,800 [[Carnival Tokens]]. If solely [[Chantelle|Carnival Goals]] were used to gain [[Carnival Tokens]], it would take 17 [[Carnival|Carnivals]] to purchase all its perks.
- Doug's perks used to be bugged and showed every value as 0. This has been fixed. [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Idk when it was fixed.; When this info is added, remove this.)

## Dialogue

[NPC] Dr. Emmett: You can learn all of the secrets through the many rooms I built.|

[NPC] Dr. Emmett: Anyway! [NPC] Dr. Emmett: In this puzzle experiment, toggle which mirror is active by clicking on it.|

|Eighth Room Selecting [The Wizard mentioned agreat danger approaching our world. What is it?]|[NPC] Dr. Emmett: This is highly sensitive information. [NPC] Dr. Emmett: It is related to the Precursors, but I cannot say more. Select an option: [Why did you choose to enter the breach?]|

I made a critical discovery, which MUST remain confidential. [NPC] Dr. Emmett: I entrusted a letter containing my findings to Lathrop for delivery. [NPC] Dr. Emmett: As the letter never reached the wizard, I had Alatar summon an exact copy of it. [NPC] Dr. Emmett: I threw the letter into the breach, which broke the laws of causality. [NPC] Dr. Emmett: I instantly appeared here, shattered by my boneheaded decision-making and eternally trapped. [NPC] Dr. Emmett: But I've been continuing my research. [NPC] Dr. Emmett: You're part of it. Select an option: [Yes. That is not weird at all.]|
|Eighth Room Selecting [Yes. That is not weird at all.]|[NPC] Dr. Emmett: May you lead a fulfilling life!|
|Eighth Room None|[NPC] Dr. Emmett: It's you again. Select an option: [Open your shop] [I have a few questions...]|
## Lore
### Story
After the [[Jythar|Calamity]] took the life of [[The Watcher|Lathrop]]'s parents, Dr. Emmett adopted him. He was interested in studying [[The Breach]] that also came from the Calamity. One day, over thirty years after the Calamity, he asked Lathrop to deliver a letter with details regarding his experiment to his colleague in the [[Wilderness]]. The letter however, was never delivered, since Lathrop went to The Catacombs with [[Necron]] and became [[The Watcher]].
Since Lathrop never delivered the letter, Dr. Emmett asked [[Alatar]] to summon a copy of it. He threw the letter into the breach, which caused an event that transformed him into the [[Rift Dimension]], to be forever stuck there. He continues his study from inside of the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] as the first human to enter this dimension, unfortunately he is forever stuck there.
## Trivia
- Dr. Emmett is one of a few NPCs who are not originally from [[Rift Dimension]], alongside [[Julian]], [[Barry]], [[Alatar]] and [[Tel Kar]].
- Dr. Emmett and [[The Watcher]]'s names are references to Dr. Emmett Lathrop Brown, also known as Doc, from Back to the Future: in the lore, The Watcher's original name was Lathrop.

# Drakuu
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Drakuu]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells the [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]] to players wearing full [[Kuudra Follower Armor]].
Location: [[The Wasteland]]
Coordinates: -324.5 191 -1015.5
Drakuu is an [[NPC]] in [[The Wasteland]] that sells the [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]] and gives out the [[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]]. They only appear after defeating [[Kuudra]] once.
## Purpose
Drakuu will open a shop menu to players wearing [[Kuudra Follower Armor]] during a [[Blazing Volcano|CATACLYSMIC]] eruption from the [[Blazing Volcano]]. In it, he sells the [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]] for 100,000,000 [[Coins]], 1,024 (16 stacks) [[Enchanted Red Sand]], 1,024 [[Enchanted Mycelium]], and 64 (1 stack) [[Kuudra Teeth]].
He will also give out the [[Kuudra Teeth Plaque|Exquisite Kuudra Teeth Plaque]] to players wearing full [[Kuudra Follower Armor]] in exchange for a [[Golden Ball]]. This can be used to upgrade the artifact to the [[Kuudra Follower Relic]], and unlock the ability to obtain the [[Kuudra Mandible]] from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Loot]].
## Dialogue

|-|-|

|Without wearing [[Kuudra Follower Armor]]|[NPC] Drakuu: There are many ways to find Kuudra, you just have to think.|
|Wearing [[Kuudra Follower Armor]] without a CATACLYSMIC ([[Blazing Volcano]]) eruption|[NPC] Drakuu: You might be someone worth taking to, but the condition isn't right.|
|Wearing [[Kuudra Follower Armor]] during a CATACLYSMIC ([[Blazing Volcano]]) eruption|[NPC] Drakuu: You seem like someone of similar interests. [NPC] Drakuu: I have a shop of some unique items that may interest you. [NPC] Drakuu: Have a look and see if theres anything you like.|

[NPC] Drakuu: In order to do so, I need something special... [NPC] Drakuu: Bring it to me and I will be ready to assist!|
|(same as above) Wearing [[Kuudra Follower Armor]], without holding a [[Golden Ball]]|[NPC] Drakuu: Adventurer! [NPC] Drakuu: I see you have something in your inventory... [NPC] Drakuu: I feel as though I've seen it before...|

## Trivia
- Drakuu is an anagram of [[Kuudra]].
- Unlike most merchants that sell only one item, Drakuu has a full shop UI.
- Prior 0.17.2, to open the shop UI, it required all players in the same lobby being in the air via either floating in midair or standing on the very edge of a block.

Duncan is an [[NPC]] located at the top of a 1x1 room in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. He sells an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Farming Exp Boost]] for 1,500,000 [[Coins]].
## Dialogue

## Trivia
- Duncan's skin is a reference to the Greek hero Heracles, or the Romanized Hero Hercules, son of Zeus/Jupiter known for his strength and his completion the Labours of Heracles. Heracles is depicted as wearing the skin of the Nemean lion which he killed with his bare hands. This lion skin is found on the skin of Duncan.

# Elizabeth
Aliases: Community Shop
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Outdated pages, Pages with UIs
[Elizabeth]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Safety First ([[Quests]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Allows the player to learn about [[City Projects]] or purchase Fire Sales ([[Fire Sale]]), [[Account & Profile Upgrades]], and Booster Cookies ([[Booster Cookie]]).
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: -6.25 79 19.25
Elizabeth is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Community Center]]. To access her associated features, players must be at least [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 3]].
## Usage
Elizabeth has many different features in her menu, such as the option to see the most recent and all past [[City Projects]] and [[Fire Sale|Fire Sales]].
Another feature is the [[Account & Profile Upgrades]] menu, where players can get bonuses for them on all of their [[Profile|profiles]], such as more [[Magic Find]] and [[Ender Chest]] pages. The player can also get bonuses for all their co-op members on one profile, some for free (like [[Minions|Minion Slots]], some costing [[SkyBlock Gems]] such as [[Co-op|Co-op slots]].
Another menu allows players to receive 1 [[Booster Cookie]] for free and then purchase additional ones for [[SkyBlock Gems]]. The user can buy 1 [[Booster Cookie]] for 325 [[SkyBlock Gems]], 6 [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]] for 1,950 [[SkyBlock Gems]], or 12 [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]] for 3,900 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
In her menu is also located the Bits Shop, where players can use [[Bits]], obtained using [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]], to purchase various items. Most notably, the player can buy a [[God Potion]] for 1,500 [[Bits]], a [[Kismet Feather]] for 1,350 [[Bits]], [[Pure Black Dye]] and [[Pure White Dye]] for 250,000 [[Bits]], and [[Enrichments]] for 5,000 [[Bits]]
Her last menu lists the 5 commonly available [[Ranks]] on Hypixel and their perks: 2[[Ranks|[VIP]]], 3[[Ranks|[VIP+]]], 4[[Ranks|[MVP]]], 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]], and 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]]. Furthermore, there is an item at the bottom of this page, as well as other pages, displaying your current [[SkyBlock Gems]], [[Bits]], and [[Fame Ranks|Fame Rank]].
After completing [[Brigette]]'s account security quest, she gives the player a free [[Booster Cookie]].
