## Account and Profile Upgrades
This article needs to be reworked as its content is outdated.
You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
Reason: 2026/April 8 Changes to Skyblock Gem bundles resulting in an increase of cost
This article was last edited by Pigicial (contribs|logs|block) on 1:25, September 3 2026 (54 days ago)
These upgrades can be applied to either your current profile or your entire account. Some upgrades are free, while others require SkyBlock Gems to unlock. Each upgrade takes a certain amount of time to complete, but you can use SkyBlock Gems to skip the waiting.
To fully upgrade everything to the maximum level, a player will need 6,050 SkyBlock Gems. The total waiting time is 382 real-world days for profile upgrades and 244 days for account upgrades. You can choose to spend an additional 80,904 SkyBlock Gems to skip the waiting period entirely. The cost will depend on the current value of SkyBlock Gems.
#### Upgrades per tier of gems bought:
|Option|Bundle|Bundle Price|Gems per Pack|Packs Needed|Total Gems|Total Cost|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|1|700|$7.49|93.46 gems/$|9|6,300|$67.41|
|2|1,800|$18.74|96.05 gems/$|4|7200|$73.96|
|3|3,750|$37.49|100.03 gems/$|2|7,500|$74.98|
|4|7,600|$74.99|101.35 gems/$|1|7,600|$74.99|
|5|17,000|$149.99|113.34 gems/$|1|17,000|$149.99|
The best option is to buy 3 × 1,800 packs + 1 × 700 pack for a total of 6,100 Gems at a cost of $61,67 — but only if you plan to upgrade without skipping the waiting time.
#### Skipping time per tier of gems bought:
|Option|Bundle|Bundle Price|Gems per Pack|Packs Needed|Total Gems|Total Cost|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|1|700|$7.25|96.55 gems/$|116|81,200|$841.00|
|2|1,800|$18.14|99.23 gems/$|45|81,000|$816.30|
|3|3,750|$36,29|103.33 gems/$|22|82,500|$798.38|
|4|7,600|$72.59|104.70 gems/$|11|83,600|$798.49|
|5|17,000|$145.19|117.09 gems/$|5|85,000|$725.95|
The best option, considering you need the previous gems to upgrade and skip the waiting time, is to buy: 4 × 17,000 packs + 1 × 7,600 pack + 1 × 3,750 pack + 1 × 1,800 pack for a total of 6,300 Gems at a cost of $707.78.
## Trivia
- The patch notes for the Better Mayors update claim that "Elizabeth was too stubborn to move, so she is staying at the same location". Despite this claim, she actually moved 4 blocks.
- On the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], the Community Shop requires [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 10]] instead of [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 3]].
- When Elizabeth says "That's rough, buddy." during the Security Safety Quest; it appears to be a reference to Prince Zuko from ATLA.
## Gallery
- Elizabeth as seen in the Community Center.

# Essence Shops
Aliases: Crimson Essence Shop, Diamond Essence Shop, Dragon Essence Shop, Essence Shop, Forest Essence Shop, Fossil Essence Shop, Gold Essence Shop, Ice Essence Shop, Safari Essence Shop, Spider Essence Shop, Undead Essence Shop, Wither Essence Shop
Categories: Shops, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Essence Shops]
Essence Shops are shops found throughout [[SkyBlock]] where the player can trade [[Essence]] in exchange for stat boosts. Some Essence Shops also allow the player to purchase exclusive items for high amounts of [[Essence]].
## Essence Shops
- [[Essence Shops/Undead|Undead]]
- [[Essence Shops/Wither|Wither]]
- [[Essence Shops/Dragon|Dragon]]
- [[Essence Shops/Spider|Spider]]
- [[Essence Shops/Crimson|Crimson]]
- [[Essence Shops/Ice|Ice]]
- [[Essence Shops/Gold|Gold]]
- [[Essence Shops/Diamond|Diamond]]
- [[Essence Shops/Forest|Forest]]
- [[Essence Shops/Fossil|Fossil]]
- [[Essence Shops/Safari|Safari]]
- [[Essence Shops/Sun Gecko|Sun Gecko]]
## Maxing Out
Complete Essence Shop Costs:
|Shop|Essence|
|-|-|
|Undead ([[Undead Essence]])|540,600 Undead Essence|
|Wither ([[Wither Essence]])|33,500 Wither Essence|
|Dragon ([[Dragon Essence]])|67,275 Dragon Essence|
|Spider ([[Spider Essence]])|45,225 Spider Essence|
|Crimson ([[Crimson Essence]])|37,500 Crimson Essence|
|Ice ([[Ice Essence]])|61,100 Ice Essence|
|Gold ([[Gold Essence]])|49,300 Gold Essence|
|Diamond ([[Diamond Essence]])|44,400 Diamond Essence|
|Sun Gecko ([[Sun Gecko Essence]])|280 Sun Gecko Essence|
|Forest ([[Forest Essence]])|45,250 Forest Essence|
|Fossil ([[Fossil Essence]])|66,250 Fossil Essence|
|Safari ([[Safari Essence]])|48,350 Safari Essence|
If one were to purchase all of the [[Essence]] required from the [[Bazaar]], it would cost a total of .
  This drops to if Help of the Fairies and Resourceful are ignored.

# Fear Mongerer
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Events, Spooky Festival
[Fear Mongerer]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Sells items during the [[Spooky Festival]].
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: -32.5 69 6.5
The Fear Mongerer is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Village]] during the [[Spooky Festival]]. They sell items in exchange for [[Coins]] and Candy.
They are also in the Village for a one hour window before and after the Spooky Festival, available to trade items.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Fear Mongerer: During the Spooky Festival, you can collect Candy by killing mobs or fishing. [NPC] Fear Mongerer: I accept Candy in exchange for exclusively spooky items! [NPC] Fear Mongerer: Each Spooky Festival, the top Candy-collecting players can earn even spookier rewards! [NPC] Fear Mongerer: Click me again to open my shop.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Fear Mongerer: ✆ You want to be able to call me anytime? [NPC] Fear Mongerer: ✆ Well that's not possible, I'm only available at night. [NPC] Fear Mongerer: ✆ If you give me 64 Purple Candy and 6 Ectoplasm, I might give you my contact. [GIVE ITEMS]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Fear Mongerer: ✆ Mhm... Your face just isn't very scary... [NPC] Fear Mongerer: ✆ But it's not your fault I suppose, here take my contact.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling During Daytime|[NPC] Fear Mongerer: ✆ Listen buddy it's not because I gave you my contact that it gives you the right to wake me up in the middle of the day. [NPC] Fear Mongerer: ✆ Call me back tonight or I'm blocking you.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Fear Mongerer: Mortal, the time of reckoning is near. The Past will light the future.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Fear Mongerer: May your destiny be ridded with fortune.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Fear Mongerer: Soon.|
## Trivia
- There was a glitch, when someone was standing on the spot where the Fear Mongerer spawns (not during the spooky event), you were able to access the shop. This was supposedly patched but has been accessed during times other than during the spooky event. This glitch works with an armor stand taken in the Fear Mongerer's place.
- When using an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] to call them, the shop can be accessed even when it is not the Spooky Festival, but only at nighttime.
  - By setting the time to night in the Island Time or Garden Time menu, you can call them anytime from your [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]], regardless of the actual in-game time.

# Freddy
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Freddy]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Teaches the player how to Hunt ([[Hunting]]) certain creatures.
Location: [[Torrhus Canyon]]
Coordinates: -694.5 94.5 200.5
Freddy is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Torrhus Canyon]] who teaches the player how to [[Hunting|Hunt]] several different creatures.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Freddy: My brother Teddy and I are some of the greatest hunters who have ever lived. [NPC] Freddy: This here Torrhus Canyon has become a second home to me over the years. [NPC] Freddy: If you ever need tips on how to hunt any Critter on this here island, I’m yer man.|
|Ants ([[Ant]]) tip (5,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Ants? [NPC] Freddy: Hmm, them little fellas live down in Ant's Cave... [NPC] Freddy: That thing's deep down in Torrhus Springs. [NPC] Freddy: Be sure to bring a Vacuum with ya - you'll need it to suck 'em up.|
|Beeheemoths ([[Beeheemoth]]) tip (20,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Beeheemoths? Even I dunno where they come from. [NPC] Freddy: They just show up sometimes, yaknow? Flying high around the island. [NPC] Freddy: Listen out for a loud humming and keep yer eyes peeled and Lasso at the ready, I'd say.|
|Blue Jays ([[Blue Jay]]) tip (5,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Blue Jays? Them little guys live up in Torrhus Heights. [NPC] Freddy: Climb up high, Lasso in hand, and sneak up on 'em.|
|Bunbuns ([[Bunbun]]) tip (5,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Bunbuns? You ain't seen one yet? [NPC] Freddy: They're everywhere, hoppin' around!|
|Drybarks ([[Drybark]]) tip (15,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Them there Drybarks spawn from Helix Tree Gifts. [NPC] Freddy: Keep an eye on 'em while you get yer Lasso out. [NPC] Freddy: Don't blink, or they'll come right atcha.|
|Dustybits ([[Dustybit]]) tip (10,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Them frogs known as Dustybits leap around the Spring Path. [NPC] Freddy: Chase 'em down, following them after each leap, an' eventually they'll get too tired to run any more. [NPC] Freddy: It's a battle of attrition, I'll say, you versus the beast.|
|Embers ([[Ember]]) tip (10,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Them Embers live in Torrhus Springs, I believe. [NPC] Freddy: They be quite deep down, compared to Solars. [NPC] Freddy: Catch 'em all in yer Fishing Net.|
|Firefoxes ([[Firefox]]) tip (5,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Pop open a Helix Tree Gift, ye might find a Firefox. [NPC] Freddy: They be quite timid, and won't cause yer Lasso any problems.|
|Goldolotes ([[Goldolot]]) tip (15,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Hmm, the Goldolot. [NPC] Freddy: That be a rare mutation of the Sepialot that sometimes appears in Torrhus Springs. [NPC] Freddy: I would say, hmm, maybe a 20% chance o' replacin' it. [NPC] Freddy: Sneak up on it and catch it in yer Fishing Net.|
|Groundhogs ([[Groundhog]]) tip (5,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: If I recall, them Groundhogs spawn from Helix Tree Gifts. [NPC] Freddy: They're quite feisty, but use that there Lasso to tire 'em out.|
|Hideonsuns ([[Hideonsun]]) tip (5,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Hideonsun, hmm, sneaky buggers. [NPC] Freddy: Keep yer eyes peeled for any strange lookin' Orange Boxes. [NPC] Freddy: They blend into the environment real well. [NPC] Freddy: Once ya find one, jump on its head or give it a punch to wake it up.|
|Hivethiefs ([[Hivethief]]) tip (15,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: I seen a Hivethief or two show up sometimes. [NPC] Freddy: They come by when you smear a Helix Tree with Honeycomb. [NPC] Freddy: Me good friend Honey knows more about all that than me.|
|[[Honeybuzz]] tip (5,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Groups of Honeybuzz live within Honeyhives 'round the island. [NPC] Freddy: Stick yer hand in and you might just find one!|
|Mountain Goats ([[Mountain Goat]]) tip (20,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: The Mountain Goat live right up high. [NPC] Freddy: On the Spring Path and other tall points is where you'll find 'em. [NPC] Freddy: Keep yer distance when you Lasso 'em, though - their headbutt will send yer flying.|
|Pangolins ([[Pangolin]]) tip (15,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Them Pangolins be right scared of their own shadow, I tell ya. [NPC] Freddy: Sneak up on 'em, quiet as you can, and Lasso 'em before they can turn into a ball. [NPC] Freddy: If they do curl up, yer gonna have to wait a while until they let their guard down again.|
|[[Parched]] tip (10,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Them Parched be really thirsty. [NPC] Freddy: If you smear a Helix Tree with Honey, they might show up. [NPC] Freddy: The lovely Honey knows more about all that stuff than I do.|
|Pollendarts ([[Pollendart]]) tip (5,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Them Pollendarts love their Honey, I know that much! [NPC] Freddy: If you smear a Helix Tree with Honey, they might come. [NPC] Freddy: Miss Honey knows more about all that than I do. Ask her!|
|Pucks ([[Puck]]) tip (15,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Open up a Helix Tree Gift, ye might find a Puck. [NPC] Freddy: They be mischievous little buggers, but they ain't as strong as a well-placed Lasso, I can tell yer that much.|
|Queen Ants ([[Queen Ant]]) tip (10,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Ah, the Queen Ant. A special one, she is. [NPC] Freddy: Dive deep down into Torrhus Springs, make yer way into Ant's Cave. [NPC] Freddy: You'll rarely find her scuttlin' about on the cave floor. [NPC] Freddy: She's more likely to come out if ya start destroyin' the Anthills, though.|
|Sepialots ([[Sepialot]]) tip (10,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Sepialot, huh, that thing be up in Torrhus Springs. [NPC] Freddy: Sneak up on it and catch it in yer Fishing Net before it sees ya.|
|Tiki Trios tip (10,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Hur hur hur hurr! That Tiki Trio, I hate 'em, I really do! [NPC] Freddy: The Sneaky Tiki, Shrieky Tiki, and Cheeky Tiki. [NPC] Freddy: I can't tell ya which one I hate the most. But I can tell ya which one I hate the least - none of 'em! [NPC] Freddy: You can find 'em up in Torrhus Heights. [NPC] Freddy: They'll stay still, unmovin', until you solve their puzzle. [NPC] Freddy: Then they'll come to life and blitz 'ya. [NPC] Freddy: Fend 'em off, and catch 'em in a Pocket Black Hole. [NPC] Freddy: Ah, just thinkin 'bout them has me blood boilin'...|
|Solars ([[Solar]]) tip (10,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Eh, them Solars live in Torrhus Springs, too. [NPC] Freddy: They be needing light to function, so they be up near the surface. [NPC] Freddy: Scoop em up in yer Fishing Net.|
|Timils ([[Timil]]) tip (5,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Are ye blind, kid? [NPC] Freddy: Turn around and look into that there Pond! [NPC] Freddy: It's completely overflowin' with Timil!|
|Water Snakes ([[Water Snake]]) tip (10,000 [[Coins]])|[NPC] Freddy: Water Snakes, huh... [NPC] Freddy: They be livin in the deepest depths of Torrhus Springs. [NPC] Freddy: Get all of yer best divin' gear on, and them pay 'em a visit. [NPC] Freddy: Bring yer biggest net, too - a Turbo Fishing Net or better should do the trick.|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 29, 2026|Added Freddy.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 4, 2026|0.27|Added Freddy.|

# Frosty

|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Frosty: Welcome to the Jerry's Workshop. [NPC] Frosty: Here the Jerrys make and deliver Gifts to all of SkyBlock! [NPC] Frosty: When its time to deliver Gifts, Mount Jerry always erupts and the village is ravaged by heat-based enemies! [NPC] Frosty: It is up to you, [player], to help save the day. Take this Snow Cannon to aid you in your defense against the Magma Grinches. [NPC] Frosty: It holds and fires Snowballs, which are the best way to extinguish the enemy! [NPC] Frosty: I can refill it at any time for a modest price. Also feel free to check out my shop upstairs!|
|With Snow Cannon ([[Frosty the Snow Cannon]]) or Blaster ([[Frosty the Snow Blaster]])|[NPC] Frosty: Click me with a Snow Cannon, Snow Blaster, or Frosty the Howitzer to refill it!|

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Frozen Alex: Brrrrr, I've been stuck down here ever since I fell into the pond with Steve.|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Frozen Alex: Here, let me share some of my ice with you.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]])|Dialogue|
|First Interaction|[NPC] ✆ Hmphhh, it's absolutely freezing round here!|
|First Interaction|[NPC] ✆ I can't feel my legs, toes, or hands!|
|First Interaction|[NPC] ✆ Hey, could you get me something to heat me up a bit?|
|First Interaction|[NPC] ✆ I'll give you my ...brr... Abiphone Contact in return.|

|Giving [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]]|[NPC] ✆ Here's my ...brr... contact...|

# Gemma
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Crystal Hollows
[Gemma]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Sells perks and upgrades in exchange for [[Diamond Essence]] as part of the Diamond Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]).
Location: [[Crystal Nucleus]]
Coordinates: 475.5 106 513.5
Gemma is an [[NPC]] in the [[Crystal Nucleus]]. They have an [[Essence Shops|Essence Shop]] where the player can purchase perks using [[Diamond Essence]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[Crystal Nucleus]] First Interaction; Before completing [[Crystal Nucleus]]|[NPC] Gemma: Hello there! That's strange... you don't look like a dwarf, but you also don't look like a Goblin! [NPC] Gemma: I've not seen a human down here since... well, nevermind. [NPC] Gemma: The name's Gemma, and I run the Diamond Essence Shop, where you can trade in Diamond Essence for useful perks and upgrades! [NPC] Gemma: Show me that you know your way around. Bring back all five Crystals found throughout the Crystal Hollows and complete the Crystal Nucleus, I'll let you use my shop. [NPC] Gemma: If you're having trouble finding any of those Crystals, go take a look at the Nucleus Vault over there. [NPC] Gemma: That'll tell you where each Crystal can be found in the Dwarven Mines.|
|[[Crystal Nucleus]] None; Before completing [[Crystal Nucleus]]|[NPC] Gemma: Stuck? Go take a look at the Nucleus Vault. [NPC] Gemma: That'll tell you where each Crystal can be found in the Crystal Hollows.|
|[[Crystal Nucleus]] First Interaction; After completing [[Crystal Nucleus]]|[NPC] Gemma: Awesome! You really completed the Crystal Nucleus? [NPC] Gemma: I'll give you 25 Diamond Essence to get you started- you can find more all around the Crystal Hollows! [NPC] Gemma: Let me show you the Diamond Essence Shop!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Gemma: ✆ Hey there. [NPC] Gemma: ✆ You want my Abiphone Contact? [NPC] Gemma: ✆ You know the signal isn't great down here, right? [NPC] Gemma: ✆ Well, I suppose it wouldn't do any harm. [NPC] Gemma: ✆ Most of the Dwarves are pretty against all of this newer tech, so I've got plenty of space in my directory. [NPC] Gemma: ✆ How about you bring me a Pure Mithril Gem? I'll trade it for my contact. [NPC] Gemma: ✆ It's such a beautiful gem. Perhaps the most beautiful one of all. [NPC] Gemma: ✆ Well, outside of the fabled Arkenstone, that is. [GIVE ITEM]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [GIVE ITEM]; without [[Pure Mithril Gem]]|[NPC] Gemma: ✆ As nice as that thing is, it's not a Pure Mithril Gem. [NPC] Gemma: ✆ If you could find me one I'd really appreciate it. [NPC] Gemma: ✆ I've heard they sometimes drop from Diamond Goblins.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving [[Pure Mithril Gem]]|[NPC] Gemma: ✆ Wow! It's beautiful!! [NPC] Gemma: ✆ Thank you so much - I think I'll fashion it into a necklace. [NPC] Gemma: ✆ Here - take my contact. ✆ Gemma has been added to your Abiphone's contacts! [NPC] Gemma: ✆ Give me a call if you ever need to use the Diamond Essence Shop!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Gemma: Oh, a human! I think...?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Gemma: Nice seeing you! It gets a bit lonely down in the Hollows.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Gemma: Maybe next time!|

# Gold Forger
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Mining
[Gold Forger]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells gold-related items.
Location: [[Gold Mine]]
Coordinates: -27.5 74 -294.5
The Gold Forger is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Gold Mine]]. They sell gold-related items.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Gold Forger: I love goooold! [NPC] Gold Forger: Click me again to open the Gold Forger Shop!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Gold Forger: Do Golden Carrots taste different than normal ones? I've actually never tried one before so I am pretty curious how they taste.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Gold Forger: Let's taste...THIS IS DISGUSTING!!! Why would anyone waste such precious gold on this? Just like any other fancy food, expensive and gross...|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Gold Forger: They would've probably tasted bad anyways...|
## Trivia
- The Gold Forger's skin, originally titled "The Golden Knight" was created by DragonsDungeon on Planet Minecraft.
- The [[Mining Merchant]] has a separate stock, meaning it is technically possible to buy up to 1,280 [[Gold Ingot|Gold Ingots]] per day.

# Golden Dragon
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Golden Dragon]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells the [[Golden Dragon Pet]].
Location: Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Crystal Hollows)]])
The Golden Dragon is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Dragon's Lair (Crystal Hollows)|Dragon's Lair]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. They sell the [[Golden Dragon Pet|Golden Dragon Egg]] that hatches into the [[Golden Dragon Pet]].
## Overview
- [[Golden Dragon Pet|Golden Dragon Egg]] can be purchased for:
  500,000,000 [[Coins]]; 50 [[Enchanted Gold Block]]; 1 [[Gemstone|Perfect Amethyst Gemstone]]; 1 [[Gemstone|Perfect Jade Gemstone]]; 1 [[Gemstone|Perfect Sapphire Gemstone]]; 1 [[Gemstone|Perfect Amber Gemstone]]; 1 [[Gemstone|Perfect Topaz Gemstone]].
- Purchasing one grants the [[Achievements|The Golden Dragon's Egg]] achievement to the player.
- There's no limit on the amount of eggs per lobby.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Golden Dragon: Do not think I am sleeping, thief. [NPC] Golden Dragon: Those eggs are mine, but I would be willing to sell them against pure gold and gemstones. [NPC] Golden Dragon: But are you rich enough?|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Golden Dragon.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Golden Dragon.|
|Sep 17, 2026|Removed the limit of the amount of Golden Dragon Eggs ([[Golden Dragon Pet]]) that can be bought in each lobby.|

# Gregory
Aliases: Gregory The Oportunist, Gregory The Opportunist, Gregory the Oportunist, Gregory the Opportunist
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, The End
[Gregory]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
[Locations]
Description: Sells a [[Decent Bow]] and 20 Flint Arrows ([[Flint Arrow]]) while an [[Ender Dragon]] is alive.
Location: [[Dragon's Nest]]
Coordinates: -607.5 22 -284.5
Description: Sells a [[Decent Bow]] and 20 Flint Arrows ([[Flint Arrow]]) while a [[Reindrake]] is alive.
Location: [[Jerry Pond]]
Coordinates: -72.5 78 28.5
Gregory is an [[NPC]] that appears in the [[Dragon's Nest]] while a [[Ender Dragon|Dragon]] fight is taking place, as well as the [[Jerry Pond]] when a [[Reindrake]] is caught. Upon these creatures' death, Gregory will leave.
## Shop
Gregory is a [[Shops|Merchant]]. He sells the following items:
|Icon|Item|Cost|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Flint Arrow]] x20|20 Flint Arrows ([[Flint Arrow]])|240 [[Coins]]|Costs around 4 times as much as buying Flint Arrows from the [[Weaponsmith]], where they cost 3.33 [[Coins]] each.|
|[[Decent Bow]]|[[Decent Bow]]|500 [[Coins]]|This Bow ([[Bows]]) is considered a ripoff as it is just an UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) bow that has no special abilities and is only slightly better than even a normal vanilla bow. However, it can be donated to the [[Museum]] for 3 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Gregory: Hey, I'm an opportunist! [NPC] Gregory: I sell Arrows and Bows for a truly overpriced amount!|
## Trivia
- Gregory's title, "The Opportunist" and his dialogue suggests that the reason he appears during enemy fights is to capitalize on the players' lack of a bow and arrows by selling them for an inflated price.
- The [[Flint Arrow|Flint Arrows]] sold by Gregory are a ripoff: they cost roughly 4 times as much as those sold by the [[Weaponsmith]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Gregory.|

# Guy
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Guy]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Allows the player to upgrade their [[Personal Bank]].
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: 51.5 78 20.5
Guy is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Village]], in a small cave, close to the [[Mountain]]. He offers players the [[Personal Bank]] and its upgrades.
## Stock
Guy provides the [[Personal Bank]] and its upgrades, granting remote access to the [[Bank]]. The upgrades shorten the cooldown between uses of the Personal Bank, maxing out at no cooldown.
|Tier|Requirements Cost|Requirements Emerald Collection|Bank Cooldown|SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]) Reward|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|I|Free|Emerald VI (6) ([[Emerald]]); ( 15,000 (234 stacks plus 24) [[Emerald]])|1 hour|None|
|II|200k [[Coins]]|Emerald VIII (8) ([[Emerald]]); ( 50,000 (781 stacks plus 16) [[Emerald]])|20 minutes|25 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|III|1M [[Coins]]|Emerald IX (9) ([[Emerald]]); ( 100,000 (1,562 stacks plus 32) [[Emerald]])|5 minutes|35 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|IV|5M [[Coins]]|250,000 (3,906 stacks plus 16) [[Emerald]]|No cooldown|50 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before Unlocking [[Personal Bank]]|[NPC] Guy: Heard of the Personal Bank? [NPC] Guy: It will let you access your bank remotely. [NPC] Guy: Unlock it through the Emerald Collection.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Guy: Heard you may got some mushrooms...I've been looking for some...|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Guy: Thank ya man, I knew I could trust you! These will come in good use!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Guy: Oh man gee, that's awful man, call me when you got 'em alright?|
## Trivia
- Guy's name references to the real life person Guy Fawkes. The mask, depicted in Guy's skin, was designed after Guy Fawkes centuries after for the graphic novel V for Vendetta.
  - The mask was designed based on the effigies of Guy Fawkes, which are ceremonially burned in Great Britain since 1606.
- Guy's skin behind his mask is a normal Steve skin with white eyes, similar to Herobrine.
- After unlocking all upgrades, the tooltip in Guy's UI will display as Hackerman.
  - This is likely a reference to the fact that Guy Fawkes mask has been used by a hacktivist group Anonymous, who use it as a symbol since 2008.

|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Added Guy.|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Moved Guy from 51.5, 79, -13.5 ([[Coordinates]]) to 51.5, 78, 20.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|

# Jacobus
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Templates with deprecated argument names, Pages with UIs
[Jacobus]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells [[Accessory Bag]] size upgrades.
Location: [[Thaumaturgist]]
Coordinates: -49.5 70 -60.5
Jacobus is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Thaumaturgist]]. He offers to increase the size of player's [[Accessory Bag]] in exchange for [[Coins]].
## Upgrades
|Upgrade|Extra Slots|Cost per +2 Slots|Cumulative|SkyBlock XP gain|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|1|2|1.5M [[Coins]]|1.5M [[Coins]]|2 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|2-5|4~10|5M [[Coins]]|21.5M [[Coins]]|4~10 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|6-10|12~20|8M [[Coins]]|61.5M [[Coins]]|12~20 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|11-20|22~40|12M [[Coins]]|181.5M [[Coins]]|22~40 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|21-99|42~198|20M [[Coins]]|1.8B [[Coins]]|42~198 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
A [[Jacobus Register]] will be rewarded to the player once they have bought 5 accessory bag upgrades, totaling to 21,500,000 [[Coins]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Interacting with Jacobus.|[NPC] Jacobus: Empty pockets never held anyone back. Only empty heads and empty hearts can do that.|
|Interacting with Jacobus.|[NPC] Jacobus: Wealth consists not in having great possessions, but in having few wants.|
|Interacting with Jacobus.|[NPC] Jacobus: Coins isn't everything, but everything needs coins.|
|Interacting with Jacobus.|[NPC] Jacobus: An investment in knowledge pays the best interest.|
|Interacting with Jacobus.|[NPC] Jacobus: The real measure of your wealth is how much you'd be worth if you lost all your coins.|
|Interacting with Jacobus.|[NPC] Jacobus: It's not the Steve who has too little, but the Steve who craves more, who is poor.|
|Interacting with Jacobus.|[NPC] Jacobus: You must gain control over your coins or the lack of it will forever control you.|
|Interacting with Jacobus.|[NPC] Jacobus: If you live for having it all, what you have is never enough.|
|Interacting with Jacobus.|[NPC] Jacobus: Coins don't buy happiness, but it helps.|
|Condition|Message|
|Trying to expand the [[Accessory Bag]] when it is already maxed|You have reached the maximum amount of Jacobus upgrades! That was NOT a smart business decision.|

# Jax
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Jax]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells higher-tier [[Arrows]].
Location: [[Archery Range]]
Coordinates: -40.5 69 -92.5
Jax is an [[NPC]] located near the [[Archery Range]] in the [[Village]] who sells arrow-related items to the player.
## Quest
After first talking to Jax, the player can go in the room to his left and start a shooting quest. The player must remain on the elevated pressure plate the entire time whilst shooting at the Redstone Lamps around the room. After completing the first quest, the player will gain access to Jax's shop. After that, the time to complete a round of shooting all the targets gets shorter, but allows players to purchase better [[Arrows]] from Jax's shop. After completing all 3 quests, Practice Mode will be unlocked, which tests how fast the [[Player]] can shoot all targets.
|Quest; Number|Time to; complete|
|-|-|
|1|25 seconds|
|2|15 seconds|
|3|12 seconds|
|4|11 seconds|
If the player triggers the 4th round, by interacting with Jax with an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] and shoots all targets under 11s, they can obtain Jax's Abiphone contact. If they can't do it in that time, they will have to pay 10,000,000 [[Coins]] for it.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Guranteed Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Jax: Hello [player]! What brings you to my workshop? I forge the newest and most powerful arrows in all of SkyBlock! [NPC] Jax: If you can prove to me you're a real archer I'll forge you arrows whenever you need them. [NPC] Jax: Ready to test your skills? Step on the pressure plate and all the targets will light up, if you can shoot them all in 25 seconds, I'll know you're the real deal.|
|Completing Quest|[NPC] Jax: Wow, you can really shoot a bow! [NPC] Jax: Well, a deal's a deal! Come to me any time and I'll forge you whatever arrows you need for adventures. [NPC] Jax: I have only one condition... These are powerful arrows, they can't fall into the wrong hands so keep them in your quiver and don't share them with anyone.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Before Completing Target Practice III|[NPC] Jax: ✆ Uh... maybe before you dare get my contact, you should prove yourself as a real archer first.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) After Completing Target Practice III|[NPC] Jax: ✆ My Abiphone contact? [NPC] Jax: ✆ Listen pal, I've been nice with you but it's strictly business etiquette. [NPC] Jax: ✆ I'm freaking Jax the Archer, you see those lanterns? I can clear them all in 11 seconds! [NPC] Jax: ✆ I'm just that good! [NPC] Jax: ✆ If you want my contact you'll have to show me you can hit them all in UNDER 11 seconds. [NPC] Jax: ✆ Otherwise you'll have to give me 10M coins to get my contact, no less. [NPC] Jax: ✆ What do you say, buddy? [PAY 10 MILLION COINS] [ATTEMPT CHALLENGE]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [ATTEMPT CHALLENGE]|[NPC] Jax: ✆ Delusional are we today? [NPC] Jax: ✆ Alright let me know when you realize the massive skill gap between us. [NPC] Jax: ✆ I unlocked Target Partice IV for you, good luck kiddo.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Completing Challenge Under 11s|[NPC] Jax: ✆ #, really??? [NPC] Jax: ✆ Did you... [NPC] Jax: ✆ I mean... [NPC] Jax: ✆ ... [NPC] Jax: ✆ wat. How?? [NPC] Jax: ✆ Take my contact and go.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Without Enough Coins|[NPC] Jax: ✆ You're poor too, huh? [NPC] Jax: ✆ Come back when you have the money.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Paying 10,000,000 [[Coins]]|[NPC] Jax: ✆ Yep, that's right, I'm the best. [NPC] Jax: ✆ Consider yourself lucky you only had to pay 10 million, I could easily have added a zero.|
## Trivia
- Jax's skin resembles a Fletcher Plains Villager from vanilla Minecraft.
- The fastest possible time at the target practice is 3.3s.

# Junker Joel
Aliases: Joel
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Article stubs
[Junker Joel]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: One Man's Trash ([[Quests]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Introduces the player to the [[Junk Sinker]] and sells items for junk.
Location: [[Backwater Bayou]]
Coordinates: 6.5 72 4.5
Junker Joel is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Backwater Bayou]] that introduces the [[Junk Sinker]] and trades junk.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Junker Joel: You alright there, my friend? [NPC] Junker Joel: I don't see many people 'round these parts. Probably the smell of the place that drives them away, I'd think. [NPC] Junker Joel: Some nasty spill happened a while ago, and ever since people 'ave been dumping their trash in the river. [NPC] Junker Joel: Anyways, if you want to help with the cleaning effort, take this Junk Sinker and get fishin'! [NPC] Junker Joel: Bring it over to Roddy and he'll help you attach it to your Fishing Rod! [NPC] Junker Joel: I'll let ya trade any Junk that you bring me for something more worth your time! [NPC] Junker Joel: Like they always say, one man's trash is another man's treasure!|
|Future Interactions|[NPC] Junker Joel: Bring any Junk you find while fishing with your Junk Sinker to me, and I'll let ya trade it in for something more worth your time!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ You doin' alright there mate? [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ Looking a little queasy. Select an option: [Do you have an Abiphone?]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting; [Do you have an Abiphone?]|[NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ Hm, what brings ya to ask? [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ You want me to put my little number thingy in it? Select an option: [Yes]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [Yes]|[NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ Well, now that I think about it, my little girl got me one for my birthday a few months back. [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ It's somewhere in my hut. Place isn't too big so it shouldn't be too hard to find. [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ However, it's only a wee little Abiphone Basic, so it ain't got many contact slots. [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ Matter of fact, I've got seven siblings alone that would probably want to be in it... [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ Hmm, it's quite the conundrum. [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ ... [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ Tell you what, here's a compromise. [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ If you can bring me a Bayou Water Orb, I'll give you my contact. [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ That thing is basically the holy grail to a junk collector like me. [GIVE ITEM]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [GIVE ITEM]; without [[Bayou Water Orb]]|[NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ That's not what I asked for! [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ If you forgot, I'm after a Bayou Water Orb! [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ Ain't nothing I'd rather have than that beautiful hunk o' junk!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving [[Bayou Water Orb]]|[NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ Well ain't she a beauty! [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ Cheers, [player name], you're always good to this old man. [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ You feel like the eighth sibling I never had. [NPC] Junker Joel: ✆ Here's my number, don't lose it! ✆ Junker Joel has been added to your Abiphone's contacts!|
## Tips
- The best upgrade order for early game gear is generally the [[Challenging Rod]], followed by the equipment pieces of [[Backwater Armor]], and finally the armor itself.
  - [[Salmon Armor]] provides better survivability, although [[Backwater Armor]] provides higher [[Sea Creature Chance]] and [[Treasure Chance]] for cheaper, along with increased damage against [[Sea Creatures]].
- Before obtaining a [[Treasure Hook]], equipping 2 pieces of [[Bronze Hunter Armor]] or better will achieve a similar effect.
## Trivia
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
- It cost 1,024 (16 stacks) [[Rusty Coin]], 124 (1 stack plus 60) [[Busted Belt Buckle]], and 12 [[Old Leather Boot]], along with 1 [[Bayou Water Orb]] and 10,000 [[Coins]] to buy each items, with the exception of the [[Treasure Bait]].
  - The [[Hermit Crab Pet]] will cost an additional 20 [[Rusty Coin]], 8 [[Busted Belt Buckle]] and 1 [[Old Leather Boot]] to upgrade to LEGENDARY.

# Kat
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Pets, Pages with math equations, 0.7.8
[Kat]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Can upgrade [[Pets]] to higher Rarities ([[Rarity]]) after a duration of time.
Location: [[Pet Care]]
Coordinates: 10.5 72 -53.5
Kat is an [[NPC]] who resides in the [[Pet Care]]. She takes care of the player's [[Pets]] for a duration of time, upgrading their rarity: cumulative experience remains the same, although the level of the pet will be adjusted accordingly. Only one pet can be taken care of at a time, and each pet has different upgrade costs and durations.
The usage of a [[Kat Flower]] or [[Kat Bouquet]] can skip 1d and 5d of the time taken to upgrade a pet.
The cost of upgrading a pet is level dependent, each level removes 0.3% of the upgrade cost.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Kat: Hey there, I'm Kat the Pet Sitter! [NPC] Kat: While you're busy adventuring I can take care of your pets. [NPC] Kat: I might even be able to give them new abilities!|
|After Upgrading A Pet|[NPC] Kat: I'll get your <pet> upgraded to <rarity> in no time! [NPC] Kat: Come back in <time> to pick it up!|
|Interaction When Upgrading A Pet|[NPC] Kat: I'm currently taking care of your <pet>! [NPC] Kat: You can pick it up in <time>. [NPC] Kat: Stop by once it's done, okay?|
|With a [[Kat Flower]]|[NPC] Kat: A flower? For me? How sweet!|
|With a [[Kat Bouquet]]|[NPC] Kat: A bouquet? For me? How sweet!|
|After Getting A Upgraded Pet|[NPC] Kat: Oh, hey! [NPC] Kat: I was able to upgrade your pet <pet> to <rarity>. [NPC] Kat: If you have any other pets you'd like to upgrade, you know where to find me!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Kat: ✆ Friends? Sure! [NPC] Kat: ✆ But hold on, I don't give my contact for everyone. [GIVE ITEM]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Without [[Kat Flower]]|[NPC] Kat: ✆ You don't have what I need! Nice try!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving [[Kat Flower]]|[NPC] Kat: ✆ Aww, you shouldn't have! :3|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Kat: ✆ Hello? [NPC] Kat: ✆ Do you want me to train your pet?|
## Pet Sitting
Upgrading higher rarity pets with Kat requires progress in the [[Taming]] skill.
| [[Taming]]; Lvl Req.|Maximum Rarity|
|-|-|
|1|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|
|5|RARE ([[Rarity]])|
|10|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|
|20|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|
|25|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|
### Costs
#### Calculator
Assuming that the factor is the same for all pets the cost is calculated as follows:
- cost=base price−0.3%×pet level
- cost=base price×(1−0.003×pet level)
"Base Price" in the calculation / calculator is based on the value in the table listed below

 param     = basePrice|Base Price|0|int

## Trivia
- Kat upgrades pets in real-time instead of [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Time]].
- Pets at higher levels will take less [[Coins]] to upgrade at Kat: the discount is 0.3% per level, and is unaffected by [[Pet Items]] and the pet's rarity.
  - Pet Items and Carrot Candy usages are not affected by Kat.
- An upgraded pet will have its level recalculated based on its cumulative experience.
- The [[Bingo Pet]], [[Grandma Wolf Pet]], [[Kuudra Pet]], [[Montezuma Pet]], and [[Wisp Pet]] cannot be upgraded by Kat and are instead upgraded through other means.
- The skin used for Kat is a reference to Serena, a deuteragonist of the anime Pokémon X/Y, and the female protagonist of the games of the same name.
- There is currently a bug that if you warp to another server before Kat finishes her dialogue and gives you the pet, you will permanently lose your pet, and you wont be able to salvage it from Jerry.
- Upgrading a pet while Kat is still talking about having upgraded your pet results in the upgrading dialogue being skipped.
- The [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] ( [[Kiwi Shard]]) Attribute implies that Kiwi is her favorite animal.

# Kat (Rift)
Aliases: Neuroscientist Kat, Vermin Bin
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Kat]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells various items in exchange for vacuuming up vermin.
Location: [[Infested House]]
Coordinates: -34.5 70 -90.5
Kat the Neuroscientist is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Infested House]].
## Shop
|Slot|Item|Cost|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Disinfestor Gloves]]|[[Disinfestor Gloves]]|12 [[Shadow Crux]]|Requires the player to vacuum 4 flies, 4 spiders and 4 silverfish to unlock for purchase.|
|[[Vermin Belt]]|[[Vermin Belt]]|1 [[Leech Belt]]|Requires the player to vacuum 15 flies and unlock the [[Disinfestor Gloves]] for purchase to unlock for purchase.|
|[[Rift Ferret Pet]]|[[Rift Ferret Pet]]|12x [[Riftwart Roots]] 3,000 [[Motes]]|Requires the player to vacuum 20 flies, 10 spiders, 20 silverfish and unlock the [[Vermin Belt]] for purchase to unlock for purchase.|
|[[Key to Infested House Soul]]|[[Key to Infested House Soul]]|500 (7 stacks plus 52) [[Motes]]|Requires the player to vacuum 40 silverfish and unlock the [[Rift Ferret Pet]] for purchase to unlock for purchase.|
|[[Fake Neuroscience Degree]]|[[Fake Neuroscience Degree]]|10x [[Volt Crux]] 10x [[Shadow Crux]] 5,000 [[Motes]]|Requires the player to vacuum 30 flies, 40 spiders, 60 silverfish and unlock the [[Key to Infested House Soul]] for purchase to unlock for purchase.|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Kat: Hey [player]! [NPC] Kat: It's so great you're here! [NPC] Kat: I was hired to disinfest this house from the vermin. Select an option: [Okay?]|
|Selecting [Okay?]|[NPC] Kat: Well, I can't do it myself! [NPC] Kat: Turns out, I'm scared of insects. [NPC] Kat: And I'm allergic to dust. [NPC] Kat: And the cleaning contract was badly negotiated. [NPC] Kat: And I have to watch over the disinfestor crew. [NPC] Kat: And... Select an option: [Wait you're scared of insects?] ]|
|Selecting [Wait you're scared of insects?]|[NPC] Kat: Yes, I suffer from arachnophobia, entomophobia and pteronarcophobia. Select an option: [Don't you petsit spiders and bees?]|
|Selecting [Don't you petsit spiders and bees?]|[NPC] Kat: You're thinking of Kat the Pet Sitter. [NPC] Kat: I'm Kat the Neuroscientist. I'm purple Kat! Select an option: [Then why were you hired to disinfest?]|
|Selecting [Then why were you hired to disinfest?]|[NPC] Kat: Quit stalling! [NPC] Kat: Am I being investigated? [NPC] Kat: You have to do this quest! [NPC] Kat: Take my vacuum cleaner.|
|After claiming the [[Turbomax Vacuum Cleaner]]|[NPC] Kat: Now please deal with the infestation problem. [NPC] Kat: Vacuum the silverfishes, spiders and flies in the area. [NPC] Kat: Make sure to empty the vacuum in the Vermin Bin!|
|Without owning a [[Turbomax Vacuum Cleaner]]|[NPC] Kat: How did you lose my vacuum? [NPC] Kat: Ok... take this replacement...|
|Purchasing the [[Disinfestor Gloves]]|[NPC] Kat: It's always good to be well equipped for the job ahead!|
|Purchasing the [[Rift Ferret Pet]]|[NPC] Kat: We found this Ferret when cleaning the house. [NPC] Kat: It's very scared and of course I don't know how to deal with pets. [NPC] Kat: I think you should bring it with you to the overworld!|
|Purchasing the [[Fake Neuroscience Degree]]|[NPC] Kat: I've had this pile of unused diplomas for a while. [NPC] Kat: Don't ask me why and let's keep it between us.|
|Clicking Enigma Soul ([[Enigma Souls]]) without [[Key to Infested House Soul]]|[NPC] Kat: Hey you can't click that yet! [NPC] Kat: Help me with the disinfestation and I'll give you a key!|

# Kay
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Kay]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Gives out the [[Very Scientific Paper]] or [[Motes]] for Lil' Pads ([[Lil' Pad]]).
Location: [[Lagoon Hut]]
Coordinates: -191 68 58.5
Kay is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Lagoon Hut]]. She gives the [[Very Scientific Paper]], which given be given to [[Dr. Edwin]], as well as lets the player sell [[Lil' Pad|Lil' Pads]] or be launched to the shore.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Kay: Who's Dr. Hibble? Never heard of them. Select an option: [He wears a gaslight on his head.]|
|Selecting [He wears a gaslight on his head.]|[NPC] Kay: Oh you mean Dr. Edwin? [NPC] Kay: Yeah! I finished the research paper!|
|None|[NPC] Kay: Wow, you made it through the lagoon! [NPC] Kay: We've been stuck here forever!|
|Being tossed to the shore|[NPC] Kay: Yeet!|
|Being sent back from the [[Leeches Lair]]|[NPC] Kay: Hey there.|
## Trivia
- Kay appears to be another version of [[Researcher Kay]] from the [[Lotus Atoll]], due to the similarities in their names, dialogue, and skins.
## History

|May 4, 2023|Added Kay.|

# Kiara
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Kiara]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Sells the [[Viper Shard]], [[Crocodile Shard]], [[Eel Shard]], and [[Gecko Shard]].
Location: [[North Reaches]]
Coordinates: -654 113 -70
Kiara is an [[NPC]] found in the [[North Reaches]]. They teach the player about Reptile and Amphibian [[Attributes|Shards]]. They also sell [[Viper Shard|Viper Shards]], [[Crocodile Shard|Crocodile Shards]], [[Eel Shard|Eel Shards]], and [[Gecko Shard|Gecko Shards]].
## Abiphone Contact
Kiara's [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact can be obtained by giving them a [[Chameleon Shard]]. Obtaining their contact increases the daily stock of their shop by +1.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Kiara: Hello! [NPC] Kiara: Are you a fan of Reptiles and Amphibians? I can't get enough of them! [NPC] Kiara: They're so precious and cute! [NPC] Kiara: They can be very hard to find, but they all have unique abilities. [NPC] Kiara: Their genetics differ from most other creatures. [NPC] Kiara: In the Fusion Machine, fusing shards with a Reptile often yields powerful results.|
|On clicking with Abiphone|[NPC] Kiara: ✆ Oh hey, I've seen you around. [NPC] Kiara: ✆ I've been trying to get my Abiphone updated with more local contacts. [NPC] Kiara: ✆ But I don't just hand out my number to anyone. You've got to earn it. Select an option: [Of course.]|
|On clicking [Of course.]|[NPC] Kiara: ✆ There's this creature I've been dying to track. It shifts colors, blends in, vanishes like mist. [NPC] Kiara: ✆ They say if you're lucky, you might find one in Tree Gifts... a Chameleon Shard. [NPC] Kiara: ✆ Bring me one. If you can do that, maybe you're worth talking to. [GIVE SHARD]|
|On clicking [GIVE SHARD] without a [[Chameleon Shard]].|[NPC] Kiara: ✆ You don't have what I need! Nice try!|
|On clicking [GIVE SHARD] with a [[Chameleon Shard]].|[NPC] Kiara: ✆ You actually found one? You weren't kidding around. [NPC] Kiara: ✆ Look at those colors... even holding it feels like it's trying to disappear. [NPC] Kiara: ✆ Alright, fair's fair. I'm adding you to my Abiphone. [NPC] Kiara: ✆ Just don't flood me with calls. ✆ Kiara has been added to your Abiphone's contacts!|
## Trivia
- [[Diaz]]'s Shopping Spree perk does not apply to Kiara's shop.
- It costs 66,400,000 [[Coins]] and 7, 8, 8 and 12 days to upgrade each [[Attributes|Attribute]] sold to level X.
  - This can be reduced to 6, 7, 7 and 10 days with Kiara's [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]].
  - This can be reduced up to 64,408,000 [[Coins]] with [[Seal of the Family]] accessory line.

Lazarus is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Shifted Tavern]].

|Condition|Dialogue|

|Buying [[Chester]]'s Tip|[NPC] Lazarus: I don't know who the supplier is, but I know someone who does. [NPC] Lazarus: I can tell you their name for 20 Motes. Select an option: [Buy tip]|
